## `NR1L-PowerManagement-001`　`SWE-PM-071`　Splash screen shown after SplashScreen_Time on normal boot

**Test Item**

```
TLM boot requires following timings:
After SplashScreen_Time the splash screen is loaded and shown on TLM display (only if TLM has not to pass to Standby status nor to Bench status: in these cases no splash screen has to be shown);
 After StandardScreen_Time the standard screen is visualized on TLM screen

(read the TLM display before and after SplashScreen_Time -> No splash screen appears before SplashScreen_Time has elapsed, and the splash screen is loaded once it has)
```

**Pre-Conditions**

```
1. A suspend-resume boot sequence is available on the bench
```

**Input Test Data**

```
NA
```

**Test procedure**

```
1. Start the suspend-resume boot sequence
2. PENDING: DR-PW30 SplashScreen_Time 之值 —— read the HU screen and check the "Splash Screen" against that time on the recording
```

**Expected Result**

```
1. The TLM display stays blank while the boot sequence runs
2. No "Splash Screen" appears before SplashScreen_Time has elapsed, and the "Splash Screen" is loaded once it has
```

**Remarks**

```
(R-P394 B5；規則 無) (R-P396 字典套用)
```

## `NR1L-PowerManagement-002`　`SWE-PM-071`　No splash screen when TLM passes to Standby

**Test Item**

```
TLM boot requires following timings:
After SplashScreen_Time the splash screen is loaded and shown on TLM display (only if TLM has not to pass to Standby status nor to Bench status: in these cases no splash screen has to be shown);
 After StandardScreen_Time the standard screen is visualized on TLM screen

(read the TLM display through SplashScreen_Time -> No splash screen appears at any time through SplashScreen_Time, and the TLM reaches the Standby boot target)
```

**Pre-Conditions**

```
1. A suspend-resume boot sequence is available on the bench
```

**Input Test Data**

```
NA
```

**Test procedure**

```
1. Set the boot target status to Standby and start the suspend-resume boot sequence
2. PENDING: DR-PW30 SplashScreen_Time 之值 —— read the HU screen and check the "Splash Screen" against that time on the recording
```

**Expected Result**

```
1. The TLM display stays blank while the boot sequence runs
2. No "Splash Screen" appears at any time through SplashScreen_Time, and the TLM reaches the Standby boot target
```

**Remarks**

```
(R-P394 B5；規則 無) (R-P396 字典套用)
```

## `NR1L-PowerManagement-004`　`SWE-PM-071`　Standard screen shown after StandardScreen_Time

**Test Item**

```
TLM boot requires following timings:
After SplashScreen_Time the splash screen is loaded and shown on TLM display (only if TLM has not to pass to Standby status nor to Bench status: in these cases no splash screen has to be shown);
 After StandardScreen_Time the standard screen is visualized on TLM screen

(read the TLM screen content before and after StandardScreen_Time -> The standard screen is not visualized before StandardScreen_Time has elapsed, and it is visualized once that time has passed)
```

**Pre-Conditions**

```
1. A suspend-resume boot sequence is available on the bench
```

**Input Test Data**

```
NA
```

**Test procedure**

```
1. Start the suspend-resume boot sequence with video recording running
2. PENDING: DR-PW30 SplashScreen_Time 之值 —— read the HU screen at that time and check that the "Splash Screen" is shown on it
3. PENDING: DR-PW30 StandardScreen_Time 之值 —— read the HU screen before and after that time and check that the "Standard Screen" is shown on it only after that time
```

**Expected Result**

```
1. The boot sequence is recorded
2. PENDING: DR-PW30 SplashScreen_Time
3. PENDING: DR-PW30 StandardScreen_Time
```

**Remarks**

```
(R-P386 第一批)
```

## `NR1L-PowerManagement-005`　`SWE-PM-072`　Events during boot are buffered without loss

**Test Item**

```
Any event occurring during the boot must be recognized by TLM and then TLM has to behave and process it according to the transitions described in par. “TLM_Status.Info setting” while the boot is still completing. TLM must buffer the events and process them as soon as possible, depending on boot timings.

(read the TLM_Status transitions during the remainder of the boot -> Every buffered event is processed before the boot sequence completes and none remains pending at boot completion)
```

**Pre-Conditions**

```
1. An event injection tool is connected to the bench
```

**Input Test Data**

```
Ignition values during boot: 2 (Ignition_Off), 4 (Ignition_On), 2 (Ignition_Off)
```

**Test procedure**

```
1. Start the TLM boot sequence and send each ignition value listed in Input Test Data in turn while the boot is still completing ($STATUS_BH_BCM1.OperationalModeSts$, DR-PW26)
2. Let the boot complete
3. Read the signal $STATUS_TELEMATIC.PowerSts_Telematic$ and check that it corresponds to the last ignition value that was sent
```

**Expected Result**

```
1. Each ignition value is registered without a bus error while the boot is still completing
2. The boot completes
3. The signal value $STATUS_TELEMATIC.PowerSts_Telematic$ corresponds to the last ignition value that was sent
```

**Remarks**

```
(R-P386 第一批)
```

## `NR1L-PowerManagement-006`　`SWE-PM-073`　Load Shed limits volume and mutes TLM

**Test Item**

```
When STATUS_LIN.PN14_LS_Actv=[1h] and STATUS_LIN.PN14_LS_Lvl7= [1h] signals are received by the TLM,  the TLM shall immediately reduce the maximum volume level to 20 for Ecall/ACN/Chimes/Beeps/Alerts and if the volume was greater, send the AUD_LVL signal with the updated volume level. If Ecall/ACN/chimes mode is not active, TLM shall be muted. ICS module shall power down. Under fault condition of missing load shed signals on the CAN bus, the last values of load shed signals shall be used until load shed signal broadcast resumes. If the load shed signals do not recover, the on-going load shed action shall be maintained for the rest of current ignition key cycle. The TLM shall transfer the call(not-Ecall/ACN call)  to the head set in case a continuing call is still active

While in BODY ON or BODY OFF-TIMED mode, when TLM receives STATUS_LIN.Batt_ST_Crit=[1h], the TLM shall mimimize current withdraw while keeping only the display on and controls active for HVAC controls, and active phone for ACN. TLM shall immediately reduce the maximum volume level to 20 for Ecall/ACN/Chimes/Beeps/Alerts  and if the volume was greater, send the AUD_LVL signal with the updated volume level. If Ecall/ACN/chimes mode is not active, TLM shall be muted. The TLM shall transfer the call (not-Ecall/ACN call) to the head set in case a continuing call is still active. Unless defined otherwise, TLM shall stay in this state until either voltage out of range conditions are satisfied  or shall go back to normal behavior 10 seconds after STATUS_LIN.Batt_ST_Crit becomes [0h].

(read AUD_LVL, the audio output and the ICS power state -> The maximum volume for Ecall, ACN, chimes, beeps and alerts is reduced to 20, AUD_LVL carries the updated level, the TLM is muted and the ICS module powers down)
```

**Pre-Conditions**

```
1. The bench is an Atlantis High configuration
2. A LIN and CAN simulation tool is connected
3. Ecall, ACN and chimes modes are inactive
```

**Input Test Data**

```
NA
```

**Test procedure**

```
1. Set the TLM volume level to the starting value $STATUS_LIN.PN14_LS_Actv$ = 1 (Active)
2. $STATUS_LIN.PN14_LS_Lvl7$ = 1 (Active)
3. Starting volume level: 25
4. Send the two Load Shed signals $STATUS_LIN.PN14_LS_Actv$ = 1 (Active)
5. $STATUS_LIN.PN14_LS_Lvl7$ = 1 (Active)
6. Starting volume level: 25
7. Read AUD_LVL, the audio output and the ICS power state to check the Load Shed action
```

**Expected Result**

```
1. The TLM volume indicator shows the starting level and the audio output is unmuted
2. The TLM volume indicator shows the starting level and the audio output is unmuted
3. The TLM volume indicator shows the starting level and the audio output is unmuted
4. The TLM accepts both Load Shed signals without a bus error
5. The TLM accepts both Load Shed signals without a bus error
6. The TLM accepts both Load Shed signals without a bus error
7. The maximum volume for Ecall, ACN, chimes, beeps and alerts is reduced to 20, AUD_LVL carries the updated level, the TLM is muted and the ICS module powers down
```

**Remarks**

```
(R-P394 B5；規則 R3)
```

## `NR1L-PowerManagement-007`　`SWE-PM-073`　Load Shed signals lost: last values retained

**Test Item**

```
When STATUS_LIN.PN14_LS_Actv=[1h] and STATUS_LIN.PN14_LS_Lvl7= [1h] signals are received by the TLM,  the TLM shall immediately reduce the maximum volume level to 20 for Ecall/ACN/Chimes/Beeps/Alerts and if the volume was greater, send the AUD_LVL signal with the updated volume level. If Ecall/ACN/chimes mode is not active, TLM shall be muted. ICS module shall power down. Under fault condition of missing load shed signals on the CAN bus, the last values of load shed signals shall be used until load shed signal broadcast resumes. If the load shed signals do not recover, the on-going load shed action shall be maintained for the rest of current ignition key cycle. The TLM shall transfer the call(not-Ecall/ACN call)  to the head set in case a continuing call is still active

While in BODY ON or BODY OFF-TIMED mode, when TLM receives STATUS_LIN.Batt_ST_Crit=[1h], the TLM shall mimimize current withdraw while keeping only the display on and controls active for HVAC controls, and active phone for ACN. TLM shall immediately reduce the maximum volume level to 20 for Ecall/ACN/Chimes/Beeps/Alerts  and if the volume was greater, send the AUD_LVL signal with the updated volume level. If Ecall/ACN/chimes mode is not active, TLM shall be muted. The TLM shall transfer the call (not-Ecall/ACN call) to the head set in case a continuing call is still active. Unless defined otherwise, TLM shall stay in this state until either voltage out of range conditions are satisfied  or shall go back to normal behavior 10 seconds after STATUS_LIN.Batt_ST_Crit becomes [0h].

(keep the broadcast stopped to the end of the ignition cycle -> The Load Shed action is maintained for the rest of the current ignition key cycle)
```

**Pre-Conditions**

```
1. The bench is an Atlantis High configuration
2. A LIN and CAN simulation tool is connected
3. The Load Shed condition is already active
```

**Input Test Data**

```
NA
```

**Test procedure**

```
1. Stop the broadcast of the two Load Shed signals on the bus
2. Read the AUD_LVL signal and the audio output state
3. Keep the broadcast stopped to the end of the ignition cycle to check that Load Shed is maintained
```

**Expected Result**

```
1. The two Load Shed signals are absent from the bus trace
2. AUD_LVL still carries the reduced level and the TLM stays muted
3. The Load Shed action is maintained for the rest of the current ignition key cycle
```

**Remarks**

```
(R-P394 B5；規則 無)
```

## `NR1L-PowerManagement-008`　`SWE-PM-073`　Battery Critical minimizes draw and keeps ACN active

**Test Item**

```
When STATUS_LIN.PN14_LS_Actv=[1h] and STATUS_LIN.PN14_LS_Lvl7= [1h] signals are received by the TLM,  the TLM shall immediately reduce the maximum volume level to 20 for Ecall/ACN/Chimes/Beeps/Alerts and if the volume was greater, send the AUD_LVL signal with the updated volume level. If Ecall/ACN/chimes mode is not active, TLM shall be muted. ICS module shall power down. Under fault condition of missing load shed signals on the CAN bus, the last values of load shed signals shall be used until load shed signal broadcast resumes. If the load shed signals do not recover, the on-going load shed action shall be maintained for the rest of current ignition key cycle. The TLM shall transfer the call(not-Ecall/ACN call)  to the head set in case a continuing call is still active

While in BODY ON or BODY OFF-TIMED mode, when TLM receives STATUS_LIN.Batt_ST_Crit=[1h], the TLM shall mimimize current withdraw while keeping only the display on and controls active for HVAC controls, and active phone for ACN. TLM shall immediately reduce the maximum volume level to 20 for Ecall/ACN/Chimes/Beeps/Alerts  and if the volume was greater, send the AUD_LVL signal with the updated volume level. If Ecall/ACN/chimes mode is not active, TLM shall be muted. The TLM shall transfer the call (not-Ecall/ACN call) to the head set in case a continuing call is still active. Unless defined otherwise, TLM shall stay in this state until either voltage out of range conditions are satisfied  or shall go back to normal behavior 10 seconds after STATUS_LIN.Batt_ST_Crit becomes [0h].

(read the display, HVAC controls, ACN phone state and AUD_LVL -> The display stays on, HVAC controls and ACN phone stay active, the maximum volume for Ecall, ACN, chimes, beeps and alerts is reduced to 20 and the TLM is muted)
```


**Input Test Data**

```
NA
```

**Test procedure**

```
1. Set the TLM volume level to the starting value $STATUS_LIN.Batt_ST_Crit$ = 1 (True)
2. Starting volume level: 25
3. Send the Battery Critical signal $STATUS_LIN.Batt_ST_Crit$ = 1 (True)
4. Starting volume level: 25
5. Read the display, HVAC controls, ACN phone state and AUD_LVL to check the current minimization
```

**Expected Result**

```
1. The TLM volume indicator shows the starting level and the audio output is unmuted
2. The TLM volume indicator shows the starting level and the audio output is unmuted
3. The TLM accepts the Battery Critical signal without a bus error
4. The TLM accepts the Battery Critical signal without a bus error
5. The display stays on, HVAC controls and ACN phone stay active, the maximum volume for Ecall, ACN, chimes, beeps and alerts is reduced to 20 and the TLM is muted
```

**Remarks**

```
(R-P394 B5；規則 R3)
```

## `NR1L-PowerManagement-009`　`SWE-PM-073`　Normal operation resumes 10 seconds after recovery

**Test Item**

```
When STATUS_LIN.PN14_LS_Actv=[1h] and STATUS_LIN.PN14_LS_Lvl7= [1h] signals are received by the TLM,  the TLM shall immediately reduce the maximum volume level to 20 for Ecall/ACN/Chimes/Beeps/Alerts and if the volume was greater, send the AUD_LVL signal with the updated volume level. If Ecall/ACN/chimes mode is not active, TLM shall be muted. ICS module shall power down. Under fault condition of missing load shed signals on the CAN bus, the last values of load shed signals shall be used until load shed signal broadcast resumes. If the load shed signals do not recover, the on-going load shed action shall be maintained for the rest of current ignition key cycle. The TLM shall transfer the call(not-Ecall/ACN call)  to the head set in case a continuing call is still active

While in BODY ON or BODY OFF-TIMED mode, when TLM receives STATUS_LIN.Batt_ST_Crit=[1h], the TLM shall mimimize current withdraw while keeping only the display on and controls active for HVAC controls, and active phone for ACN. TLM shall immediately reduce the maximum volume level to 20 for Ecall/ACN/Chimes/Beeps/Alerts  and if the volume was greater, send the AUD_LVL signal with the updated volume level. If Ecall/ACN/chimes mode is not active, TLM shall be muted. The TLM shall transfer the call (not-Ecall/ACN call) to the head set in case a continuing call is still active. Unless defined otherwise, TLM shall stay in this state until either voltage out of range conditions are satisfied  or shall go back to normal behavior 10 seconds after STATUS_LIN.Batt_ST_Crit becomes [0h].

(read the volume limit before and at the end of the window -> The volume limit returns to its normal maximum and the audio output is unmuted once the measurement window has elapsed)
```

**Pre-Conditions**

```
1. A LIN and CAN simulation tool is connected
2. The Battery Critical condition is already active
```

**Input Test Data**

```
NA
```

**Test procedure**

```
1. Send the recovery signal $STATUS_LIN.Batt_ST_Crit$ = 0 (False)
2. Measurement window: 10 seconds and start a timer at the moment the signal changes
3. PENDING: DR-PW27 該規格措辭之逐字定義（原措辭見 reasoning_note）
```

**Expected Result**

```
1. The volume limit stays reduced to 20 and the TLM stays muted before the measurement window elapses
2. The volume limit stays reduced to 20 and the TLM stays muted before the measurement window elapses
3. PENDING: DR-PW27 該規格措辭之逐字定義（原措辭見 reasoning_note）
```

**Remarks**

```
(R-P394 B5；規則 R3)
```

## `NR1L-PowerManagement-010`　`SWE-PM-073`　Load Shed recovers: normal volume and audio restored

**Test Item**

```
When STATUS_LIN.PN14_LS_Actv=[1h] and STATUS_LIN.PN14_LS_Lvl7= [1h] signals are received by the TLM,  the TLM shall immediately reduce the maximum volume level to 20 for Ecall/ACN/Chimes/Beeps/Alerts and if the volume was greater, send the AUD_LVL signal with the updated volume level. If Ecall/ACN/chimes mode is not active, TLM shall be muted. ICS module shall power down. Under fault condition of missing load shed signals on the CAN bus, the last values of load shed signals shall be used until load shed signal broadcast resumes. If the load shed signals do not recover, the on-going load shed action shall be maintained for the rest of current ignition key cycle. The TLM shall transfer the call(not-Ecall/ACN call)  to the head set in case a continuing call is still active

While in BODY ON or BODY OFF-TIMED mode, when TLM receives STATUS_LIN.Batt_ST_Crit=[1h], the TLM shall mimimize current withdraw while keeping only the display on and controls active for HVAC controls, and active phone for ACN. TLM shall immediately reduce the maximum volume level to 20 for Ecall/ACN/Chimes/Beeps/Alerts  and if the volume was greater, send the AUD_LVL signal with the updated volume level. If Ecall/ACN/chimes mode is not active, TLM shall be muted. The TLM shall transfer the call (not-Ecall/ACN call) to the head set in case a continuing call is still active. Unless defined otherwise, TLM shall stay in this state until either voltage out of range conditions are satisfied  or shall go back to normal behavior 10 seconds after STATUS_LIN.Batt_ST_Crit becomes [0h].

(read the volume limit and the audio output state -> The volume limit returns to its normal maximum and the audio output is unmuted)
```

**Pre-Conditions**

```
1. The bench is an Atlantis High configuration
2. A LIN and CAN simulation tool is connected
3. The Load Shed condition is already active
```

**Input Test Data**

```
NA
```

**Test procedure**

```
1. Stop the broadcast of the two Load Shed signals on the bus
2. Resume the broadcast with the recovery values $STATUS_LIN.PN14_LS_Actv$ = 0 (Not_Active)
3. $STATUS_LIN.PN14_LS_Lvl7$ = 0 (Not_Active)
4. Read the signal $TELEMATIC_FD_13.AUD_LVL$ and check that it is the expected level
5. Read the HU speakers and check whether entertainment audio output is present on them
```

**Expected Result**

```
1. The two Load Shed signals are absent from the bus trace
2. The TLM accepts the resumed broadcast without a bus error
3. The TLM accepts the resumed broadcast without a bus error
4. PENDING: DR-PW27 該規格措辭之逐字定義（原措辭見 reasoning_note）
5. PENDING: DR-PW27 該規格措辭之逐字定義（原措辭見 reasoning_note）
```

**Remarks**

```
(R-P394 B5；規則 R3) (R-P396 字典套用)
```

## `NR1L-PowerManagement-011`　`SWE-PM-073`　Continuing call transferred to head set under Load Shed

**Test Item**

```
When STATUS_LIN.PN14_LS_Actv=[1h] and STATUS_LIN.PN14_LS_Lvl7= [1h] signals are received by the TLM,  the TLM shall immediately reduce the maximum volume level to 20 for Ecall/ACN/Chimes/Beeps/Alerts and if the volume was greater, send the AUD_LVL signal with the updated volume level. If Ecall/ACN/chimes mode is not active, TLM shall be muted. ICS module shall power down. Under fault condition of missing load shed signals on the CAN bus, the last values of load shed signals shall be used until load shed signal broadcast resumes. If the load shed signals do not recover, the on-going load shed action shall be maintained for the rest of current ignition key cycle. The TLM shall transfer the call(not-Ecall/ACN call)  to the head set in case a continuing call is still active

While in BODY ON or BODY OFF-TIMED mode, when TLM receives STATUS_LIN.Batt_ST_Crit=[1h], the TLM shall mimimize current withdraw while keeping only the display on and controls active for HVAC controls, and active phone for ACN. TLM shall immediately reduce the maximum volume level to 20 for Ecall/ACN/Chimes/Beeps/Alerts  and if the volume was greater, send the AUD_LVL signal with the updated volume level. If Ecall/ACN/chimes mode is not active, TLM shall be muted. The TLM shall transfer the call (not-Ecall/ACN call) to the head set in case a continuing call is still active. Unless defined otherwise, TLM shall stay in this state until either voltage out of range conditions are satisfied  or shall go back to normal behavior 10 seconds after STATUS_LIN.Batt_ST_Crit becomes [0h].

(read the call audio routing -> The continuing call is routed to the head set and is not dropped)
```

**Pre-Conditions**

```
1. The bench is an Atlantis High configuration
2. A LIN and CAN simulation tool is connected
3. A non-Ecall non-ACN call is active and continuing
```

**Input Test Data**

```
NA
```

**Test procedure**

```
1. Send the signal $STATUS_LIN.PN14_LS_Actv$ = 1 (Active)
2. Send the signal $STATUS_LIN.PN14_LS_Lvl7$ = 1 (Active)
3. Read the HU speakers and check that the call audio is no longer present on them
4. Read the "Call Screen" on the paired phone and check that it still shows the call as connected
```

**Expected Result**

```
1. The TLM accepts the signal without a bus error
2. The TLM accepts the second signal without a bus error
3. The call audio is no longer present on the HU speakers
4. The "Call Screen" on the paired phone still shows the call as connected
```

**Remarks**

```
(R-P386 第一批)
```

## `NR1L-PowerManagement-012`　`SWE-PM-073`　Continuing call transferred to head set under Battery Critical

**Test Item**

```
When STATUS_LIN.PN14_LS_Actv=[1h] and STATUS_LIN.PN14_LS_Lvl7= [1h] signals are received by the TLM,  the TLM shall immediately reduce the maximum volume level to 20 for Ecall/ACN/Chimes/Beeps/Alerts and if the volume was greater, send the AUD_LVL signal with the updated volume level. If Ecall/ACN/chimes mode is not active, TLM shall be muted. ICS module shall power down. Under fault condition of missing load shed signals on the CAN bus, the last values of load shed signals shall be used until load shed signal broadcast resumes. If the load shed signals do not recover, the on-going load shed action shall be maintained for the rest of current ignition key cycle. The TLM shall transfer the call(not-Ecall/ACN call)  to the head set in case a continuing call is still active

While in BODY ON or BODY OFF-TIMED mode, when TLM receives STATUS_LIN.Batt_ST_Crit=[1h], the TLM shall mimimize current withdraw while keeping only the display on and controls active for HVAC controls, and active phone for ACN. TLM shall immediately reduce the maximum volume level to 20 for Ecall/ACN/Chimes/Beeps/Alerts  and if the volume was greater, send the AUD_LVL signal with the updated volume level. If Ecall/ACN/chimes mode is not active, TLM shall be muted. The TLM shall transfer the call (not-Ecall/ACN call) to the head set in case a continuing call is still active. Unless defined otherwise, TLM shall stay in this state until either voltage out of range conditions are satisfied  or shall go back to normal behavior 10 seconds after STATUS_LIN.Batt_ST_Crit becomes [0h].

(read the call audio routing -> The continuing call is routed to the head set and is not dropped)
```

**Pre-Conditions**

```
1. A LIN and CAN simulation tool is connected
2. The TLM is in BODY ON mode
3. A non-Ecall non-ACN call is active and continuing
```

**Input Test Data**

```
NA
```

**Test procedure**

```
1. Send the signal $STATUS_LIN.Batt_ST_Crit$ = 1 (True)
2. Read the HU speakers and check that the call audio is no longer present on them
3. Read the "Call Screen" on the paired phone and check that it still shows the call as connected
```

**Expected Result**

```
1. The TLM accepts the signal without a bus error
2. The call audio is no longer present on the HU speakers
3. The "Call Screen" on the paired phone still shows the call as connected
```

**Remarks**

```
(R-P386 第一批)
```

## `NR1L-PowerManagement-013`　`SWE-PM-073`　Battery Critical minimizes draw in BODY OFF-TIMED mode

**Test Item**

```
When STATUS_LIN.PN14_LS_Actv=[1h] and STATUS_LIN.PN14_LS_Lvl7= [1h] signals are received by the TLM,  the TLM shall immediately reduce the maximum volume level to 20 for Ecall/ACN/Chimes/Beeps/Alerts and if the volume was greater, send the AUD_LVL signal with the updated volume level. If Ecall/ACN/chimes mode is not active, TLM shall be muted. ICS module shall power down. Under fault condition of missing load shed signals on the CAN bus, the last values of load shed signals shall be used until load shed signal broadcast resumes. If the load shed signals do not recover, the on-going load shed action shall be maintained for the rest of current ignition key cycle. The TLM shall transfer the call(not-Ecall/ACN call)  to the head set in case a continuing call is still active

While in BODY ON or BODY OFF-TIMED mode, when TLM receives STATUS_LIN.Batt_ST_Crit=[1h], the TLM shall mimimize current withdraw while keeping only the display on and controls active for HVAC controls, and active phone for ACN. TLM shall immediately reduce the maximum volume level to 20 for Ecall/ACN/Chimes/Beeps/Alerts  and if the volume was greater, send the AUD_LVL signal with the updated volume level. If Ecall/ACN/chimes mode is not active, TLM shall be muted. The TLM shall transfer the call (not-Ecall/ACN call) to the head set in case a continuing call is still active. Unless defined otherwise, TLM shall stay in this state until either voltage out of range conditions are satisfied  or shall go back to normal behavior 10 seconds after STATUS_LIN.Batt_ST_Crit becomes [0h].

(read the display, HVAC controls, ACN phone state and AUD_LVL -> The display stays on, HVAC controls and ACN phone stay active, the maximum volume for Ecall, ACN, chimes, beeps and alerts is reduced to 20 and the TLM is muted)
```

**Pre-Conditions**

```
1. A LIN and CAN simulation tool is connected
2. The TLM is in BODY OFF-TIMED mode
3. Ecall, ACN and chimes modes are inactive
```

**Input Test Data**

```
NA
```

**Test procedure**

```
1. Set the TLM volume level to the starting value $STATUS_LIN.Batt_ST_Crit$ = 1 (True)
2. Starting volume level: 25
3. Send the Battery Critical signal $STATUS_LIN.Batt_ST_Crit$ = 1 (True)
4. Starting volume level: 25
5. Read the display, HVAC controls, ACN phone state and AUD_LVL to check the current minimization
```

**Expected Result**

```
1. The TLM volume indicator shows the starting level and the audio output is unmuted
2. The TLM volume indicator shows the starting level and the audio output is unmuted
3. The TLM accepts the Battery Critical signal without a bus error
4. The TLM accepts the Battery Critical signal without a bus error
5. The display stays on, HVAC controls and ACN phone stay active, the maximum volume for Ecall, ACN, chimes, beeps and alerts is reduced to 20 and the TLM is muted
```

**Remarks**

```
(R-P394 B5；規則 R3)
```

## `NR1L-PowerManagement-014`　`SWE-PM-073`　Battery Critical exits on voltage out of range condition

**Test Item**

```
When STATUS_LIN.PN14_LS_Actv=[1h] and STATUS_LIN.PN14_LS_Lvl7= [1h] signals are received by the TLM,  the TLM shall immediately reduce the maximum volume level to 20 for Ecall/ACN/Chimes/Beeps/Alerts and if the volume was greater, send the AUD_LVL signal with the updated volume level. If Ecall/ACN/chimes mode is not active, TLM shall be muted. ICS module shall power down. Under fault condition of missing load shed signals on the CAN bus, the last values of load shed signals shall be used until load shed signal broadcast resumes. If the load shed signals do not recover, the on-going load shed action shall be maintained for the rest of current ignition key cycle. The TLM shall transfer the call(not-Ecall/ACN call)  to the head set in case a continuing call is still active

While in BODY ON or BODY OFF-TIMED mode, when TLM receives STATUS_LIN.Batt_ST_Crit=[1h], the TLM shall mimimize current withdraw while keeping only the display on and controls active for HVAC controls, and active phone for ACN. TLM shall immediately reduce the maximum volume level to 20 for Ecall/ACN/Chimes/Beeps/Alerts  and if the volume was greater, send the AUD_LVL signal with the updated volume level. If Ecall/ACN/chimes mode is not active, TLM shall be muted. The TLM shall transfer the call (not-Ecall/ACN call) to the head set in case a continuing call is still active. Unless defined otherwise, TLM shall stay in this state until either voltage out of range conditions are satisfied  or shall go back to normal behavior 10 seconds after STATUS_LIN.Batt_ST_Crit becomes [0h].

(read the volume limit and the audio output state -> The TLM leaves the Battery Critical state without waiting for the signal to clear)
```

**Pre-Conditions**

```
1. A LIN and CAN simulation tool is connected
2. The Battery Critical condition is already active
3. The vehicle voltage out of range condition can be applied on the bench
```

**Input Test Data**

```
NA
```

**Test procedure**

```
1. Keep the Battery Critical signal at the value $STATUS_LIN.Batt_ST_Crit$ = 1 (True) (held)
2. Apply the voltage out of range condition on the bench
3. Read the signal $TELEMATIC_FD_13.AUD_LVL$ and check that it is the expected level
4. Read the HU speakers and check whether entertainment audio output is present on them
```

**Expected Result**

```
1. The TLM stays in the Battery Critical state while the signal is held
2. The TLM registers the voltage out of range condition
3. The TLM leaves the Battery Critical state without waiting for the signal to clear
4. The TLM leaves the Battery Critical state without waiting for the signal to clear
```

**Remarks**

```
DR-PW8 (High) —— `4942354` 未載 voltage out of range 之電壓門檻值；本條在取得該門檻前不可實際執行 (R-P394 B5；規則 無) (R-P396 字典套用)
```

```
When STATUS_LIN.PN14_LS_Actv=[1h] and STATUS_LIN.PN14_LS_Lvl7= [1h] signals are received by the TLM,  the TLM shall immediately reduce the maximum volume level to 20 for Ecall/ACN/Chimes/Beeps/Alerts and if the volume was greater, send the AUD_LVL signal with the updated volume level. If Ecall/ACN/chimes mode is not active, TLM shall be muted. ICS module shall power down. Under fault condition of missing load shed signals on the CAN bus, the last values of load shed signals shall be used until load shed signal broadcast resumes. If the load shed signals do not recover, the on-going load shed action shall be maintained for the rest of current ignition key cycle. The TLM shall transfer the call(not-Ecall/ACN call)  to the head set in case a continuing call is still active
```

**Expected Result**

```
2. The TLM volume indicator shows the starting level and the audio output is unmuted

4. The TLM accepts both Load Shed signals without a bus error
5. The TLM accepts both Load Shed signals without a bus error
6. The TLM accepts both Load Shed signals without a bus error
7. No AUD_LVL signal carrying a new volume level appears in the trace and the volume level is unchanged
```

**Remarks**

```
(R-P394 B5；規則 R3)
```

## `NR1L-PowerManagement-027`　`SWE-PM-063`　Bluetooth calls can be made and received in Timed state

**Test Item**

```
In Timed state, it is possible either to make and to receive one or more bluetooth phone calls according to following logics that depend on Timeout1 and on MaxCallTimeout time parameters.

(read the call audio routing and the TLM state -> Both calls were served and the TLM remains in Timed state)
```

**Pre-Conditions**

```
1. A paired bluetooth phone is available on the bench
2. The TLM is in Timed state
```

**Input Test Data**

```
NA
```

**Test procedure**

```
1. Place an outgoing bluetooth call from the paired phone through the TLM
2. Read the paired phone screen and check that it shows the call as connected, and check that the call audio is present on the HU speakers
3. End that call and let the paired phone place an incoming call to the HU
4. Read the HU screen and check that the "Incoming Call" pop-up is shown on it, then answer it
5. Read the signal $STATUS_TELEMATIC.PowerSts_Telematic$ and check that it is 2 (Timed)
```

**Expected Result**

```
1. The outgoing call is placed from the paired phone
2. The paired phone shows the call as connected and the call audio is present on the HU speakers
3. The incoming call reaches the HU
4. The "Incoming Call" pop-up is shown on the HU screen and the call is answered
5. The signal value $STATUS_TELEMATIC.PowerSts_Telematic$ = 2 (Timed) is received
```

**Remarks**

```
(R-P383)
```

## `NR1L-PowerManagement-028`　`SWE-PM-064`　MaxCallTimeout starts on ignition off with Timeout1 at 00 min

**Test Item**

```
MaxCallTimeout starts in the following two conditions: Timeout1 == 00 min: IF Phone_Call.Info is equal to “Active” in TLM Full-Operation state, AND the Ignition working condition switches to "Ignition Pre Off" OR to "Ignition Off";   Timeout1 <> 00 min: at Timeout1 expiration, only IF Phone_Call.Info is still equal to “Active”;

(read the MaxCallTimeout counter -> The MaxCallTimeout counter is running from the moment of the ignition change)
```

**Pre-Conditions**

```
1. Timeout1 is at "00 min"
2. Apply ENTER_FULL_OPERATION
3. PENDING: DR-PW23 Phone_Call.Info
```

**Input Test Data**

```
NA
```

**Test procedure**

```
1. Switch the ignition working condition to the value Ignition working condition: "Ignition Pre Off"
2. Read the MaxCallTimeout counter to check that it started
```

**Expected Result**

```
1. The TLM leaves Full-Operation state without dropping the active call
2. The MaxCallTimeout counter is running from the moment of the ignition change
```

**Remarks**

```
(R-P394 B5；規則 R5)
```

## `NR1L-PowerManagement-029`　`SWE-PM-064`　MaxCallTimeout starts at Timeout1 expiry with the call still active

**Test Item**

```
MaxCallTimeout starts in the following two conditions: Timeout1 == 00 min: IF Phone_Call.Info is equal to “Active” in TLM Full-Operation state, AND the Ignition working condition switches to "Ignition Pre Off" OR to "Ignition Off";   Timeout1 <> 00 min: at Timeout1 expiration, only IF Phone_Call.Info is still equal to “Active”;

(read the MaxCallTimeout counter at Timeout1 expiration -> The MaxCallTimeout counter is running from the moment of Timeout1 expiration)
```

**Pre-Conditions**

```
1. Timeout1 is at a value other than "00 min"
2. Apply ENTER_TIMED
3. PENDING: DR-PW23 Phone_Call.Info
```

**Input Test Data**

```
NA
```

**Test procedure**

```
1. Let Timeout1 run to its expiration while the call stays active
2. Read the MaxCallTimeout counter at Timeout1 expiration to check that it started
```

**Expected Result**

```
1. PENDING: DR-PW23 Phone_Call.Info
2. The MaxCallTimeout counter is running from the moment of Timeout1 expiration
```

**Remarks**

```
(R-P394 B5；規則 R4/R5)
```

## `NR1L-PowerManagement-030`　`SWE-PM-065`　Call ends before Timeout1 expiry: previous source is restored

**Test Item**

```
Case 1:IF Timeout1 <> 00 minutesAND IF Phone_Call.Info passes to "Not_Active" before "Timeout1” expiration THEN TLM has to restore the active source managed by TLM before the call (for example entertainment features like DAB Tuner, rather than USB or BT streaming audio) staying still in Timed state.

(read the active audio source and the TLM state -> The DAB Tuner source is active again and the TLM remains in Timed state)
```

**Pre-Conditions**

```
1. Timeout1 is at a value other than "00 min"
2. Apply ENTER_TIMED
3. A DAB Tuner source was active before the call
4. PENDING: DR-PW23 Phone_Call.Info
```

**Input Test Data**

```
NA
```

**Test procedure**

```
1. PENDING: DR-PW23 Phone_Call.Info
2. Read the source indicator and record its value, then check it against the recorded value
3. Read the signal $STATUS_TELEMATIC.PowerSts_Telematic$ and check that it is the expected 2 (Timed)
```

**Expected Result**

```
1. PENDING: DR-PW23 Phone_Call.Info
2. The DAB Tuner source is active again and the TLM remains in Timed state
3. The DAB Tuner source is active again and the TLM remains in Timed state
```

**Remarks**

```
(R-P394 B5；規則 R4/R5) (R-P396 字典套用)
```

## `NR1L-PowerManagement-031`　`SWE-PM-065`　Further calls are still managed within Timeout1

**Test Item**

```
In this case, TLM is still able to manage other possible phone calls within Timeout1 expiration.

(read the call audio routing and the TLM state -> The TLM remains in Timed state while the second call runs)
```

**Pre-Conditions**

```
1. A paired bluetooth phone is available on the bench
2. Apply ENTER_TIMED
3. $BCM_FD_27.Comfort_Enable_Time$ is at a value other than 0 (DR-PW26)
4. One call has already ended before that value has elapsed
```

**Input Test Data**

```
NA
```

**Test procedure**

```
1. Place a second bluetooth call from the paired phone while $BCM_FD_27.Comfort_Enable_Time$ has not yet elapsed
2. Answer the call and check that the call audio is present on the HU speakers
3. Read the signal $STATUS_TELEMATIC.PowerSts_Telematic$ and check that it is 2 (Timed)
```

**Expected Result**

```
1. The second bluetooth call reaches the HU before $BCM_FD_27.Comfort_Enable_Time$ has elapsed
2. The call is answered and the call audio is present on the HU speakers
3. The signal value $STATUS_TELEMATIC.PowerSts_Telematic$ = 2 (Timed) is received
```

**Remarks**

```
(R-P383)
```

## `NR1L-PowerManagement-032`　`SWE-PM-038`　Case 1 with RemStartFail true: TLM stops and passes to Standby

**Test Item**

```
IF RemStartFail = ”True” TLM has to stop its active functionality (Media audio streaming, tuner, etc) and has to set RemStartFail  to “False” value and  TLM_Status.Info and $Telematic_Power$ to “Standby” value and it passes to Standby state

(read the active functionality, RemStartFail, TLM_Status.Info and $Telematic_Power$ -> RemStartFail reads "False", TLM_Status.Info and $Telematic_Power$ read "Standby" and the TLM is in Standby state)
```

**Pre-Conditions**

```
1. Timeout1 is at a value other than "00 min"
2. The TLM is in Timed state with media audio streaming active
3. PENDING: DR-PW23 RemStartFail
4. PENDING: DR-PW23 Phone_Call.Info
```

**Input Test Data**

```
NA
```

**Test procedure**

```
1. PENDING: DR-PW23 Phone_Call.Info
2. PENDING: DR-PW23 RemStartFail
```

**Expected Result**

```
1. The media audio streaming stops and no source stays active
2. PENDING: DR-PW23 RemStartFail
```

**Remarks**

```
DR-PW10 待範圍確認 (R-P394 B5；規則 R4/R5)
```


```
1. The DAB Tuner source is active again and the TLM remains in Timed state
```


## `NR1L-PowerManagement-043`　`SWE-PM-011`　VR button press in IDLE mode transitions the HU to Full-Operation

**Test Item**

```
While the HU is in IDLE mode, the HU shall transition to Full-Operation mode if the VR button is pressed. Refer to CFTS042 for VR button press definition.

(read the HU mode -> The HU is in Full-Operation mode)
```

**Pre-Conditions**

```
1. Apply ENTER_IDLE
2. A VR button is available on the bench
```

**Input Test Data**

```
NA
```

**Test procedure**

```
1. Press the VR button with a short press and release it
2. Read the signal $STATUS_TELEMATIC.PowerSts_Telematic$ and check that it is 4 (Full_Operation)
```

**Expected Result**

```
1. The HU accepts the VR button press
2. The HU is in Full-Operation mode
```

**Remarks**

```
(R-P394 B5；規則 R2)
```

## `NR1L-PowerManagement-044`　`SWE-PM-011`　CarPlay requesting audio and video keeps audio unmuted and screen on

**Test Item**

```
If the CarPlay Device requests audio control and video control, then the HU shall remain in FULL OPERATION mode with the entertainment audio unmuted and the screen on.

(read the HU mode, the entertainment audio and the screen -> The entertainment audio is unmuted and the screen is on)
```

**Pre-Conditions**

```
1. Apply ENTER_FULL_OPERATION
2. A CarPlay Device is paired on the bench
3. A CarPlay VR interaction has completed
```

**Input Test Data**

```
NA
```

**Test procedure**

```
1. Let the CarPlay Device issue the request CarPlay request: audio control and video control
2. Read the signal $STATUS_TELEMATIC.PowerSts_Telematic$ and check that it is the expected 4 (Full_Operation)
3. Read the HU speakers and check whether entertainment audio output is present on them
4. Apply FUNC_STATE_Full_Operation and check its Display sub-item
```

**Expected Result**

```
1. The HU accepts the request without leaving FULL OPERATION mode
2. The entertainment audio is unmuted and the screen is on
3. The entertainment audio is unmuted and the screen is on
4. The entertainment audio is unmuted and the screen is on
```

**Remarks**

```
(R-P394 B5；規則 無) (R-P396 字典套用)
```

## `NR1L-PowerManagement-049`　`SWE-PM-012`　User settings are restored after a battery reconnection

**Test Item**

```
After a battery reconnection and also when TLM has to exit INIT state (as soon as the voltage is limited within certain thresholds), TLM is able to work properly again and it has to restore the last user settings and the last variables values: VPLastStatus, SwitchOffSetting.Req, Auto_SwitchOn_Setting.Req shall be restored to their values before the battery disconnection / battery reset

(read the three stored variables -> VPLastStatus, SwitchOffSetting.Req and Auto_SwitchOn_Setting.Req read their values before the battery disconnection)
```

**Pre-Conditions**

```
1. A LIN and CAN simulation tool is connected
2. VPLastStatus, SwitchOffSetting.Req and Auto_SwitchOn_Setting.Req hold known values
3. The battery is disconnected
```

**Input Test Data**

```
NA
```

**Test procedure**

```
1. Apply ENTER_FULL_OPERATION and read the signal $STATUS_TELEMATIC.PowerSts_Telematic$, recording the value as State_before
2. Disconnect the battery from the HU, then reconnect it
3. Send the signal $STATUS_BH_BCM1.OperationalModeSts$ = 4 (Ignition_On) (DR-PW26), then read the signal $STATUS_TELEMATIC.PowerSts_Telematic$ and check that it equals State_before
4. PENDING: DR-PW25 SwitchOffSetting.Req 與 Auto_SwitchOn_Setting.Req 之設定項名與讀取方法
```

**Expected Result**

```
1. State_before is recorded
2. The battery is disconnected and reconnected
3. The signal value $STATUS_TELEMATIC.PowerSts_Telematic$ equals State_before
4. PENDING: DR-PW25 SwitchOffSetting.Req / Auto_SwitchOn_Setting.Req
```

**Remarks**

```
(R-P386 第一批)
```

## `NR1L-PowerManagement-050`　`SWE-PM-012`　TLM starts from Sleep state after leaving INIT

**Test Item**

```
Then, TLM has to behave according to requirements of par. "TLM_Status.Info and $Telematic_Power$ signal setting", setting TLM_Status.Info to "Sleep" first and starting from Sleep state.

(read TLM_Status.Info and the state machine -> TLM_Status.Info reads "Sleep" and the TLM starts from Sleep state)
```

**Pre-Conditions**

```
1. A LIN and CAN simulation tool is connected
2. The battery has just been reconnected
```

**Input Test Data**

```
NA
```

**Test procedure**

```
1. Disconnect the battery from the HU
2. Reconnect the battery
3. Read the bus trace and check that the first $STATUS_TELEMATIC$ frame transmitted after the reconnection carries $STATUS_TELEMATIC.PowerSts_Telematic$ = 0 (Sleep)
```

**Expected Result**

```
1. The battery is disconnected
2. The battery is reconnected
3. The first $STATUS_TELEMATIC$ frame after the reconnection carries $STATUS_TELEMATIC.PowerSts_Telematic$ = 0 (Sleep)
```

**Remarks**

```
(R-P386 第一批)
```

## `NR1L-PowerManagement-055`　`SWE-PM-013`　AMP, ICS and DTV are off while chime audio stays active

**Test Item**

```
This status is related to TLM OFF. AMP/ICS/DTV shall be OFF. Audio for ANC, ACN, and chimes (if equipped) shall be active in this state)

(read the AMP, ICS and DTV power states and the audio paths -> AMP, ICS and DTV are OFF while audio for ANC, ACN and chimes is active)
```

**Pre-Conditions**

```
1. A LIN and CAN simulation tool is connected
2. The TLM is in Partial Operation with AMP, ICS and DTV equipped
```

**Input Test Data**

```
NA
```

**Test procedure**

```
1. Apply ENTER_PARTIAL_OPERATION
2. Read the signal $STATUS_TELEMATIC.PowerSts_Telematic$ and check that it is 7 (Partial_Operation)
3. Apply FUNC_STATE_PARTIAL_OPERATION and check each of its sub-items
4. Send the signal $PARK_INFO.ChimeActivation_LHF$ = 1 (Active) and check that the chime is audible on the left hand front speaker
```

**Expected Result**

```
1. The HU is in Partial Operation
2. The signal value $STATUS_TELEMATIC.PowerSts_Telematic$ = 7 (Partial_Operation) is received
3. FUNC_STATE_PARTIAL_OPERATION holds:
   a. No audio source is playing and no amplifier output is present
   b. The HU display is off, except for the HMI Antitheft Screens (CFTS009-4941457)
   c. PENDING: DR-PW27 BoosterOUT OFF / antenna supply ON 位準值
   d. A USB device is not enumerated and the AUX input does not play
4. The chime is audible on the left hand front speaker
```

**Remarks**

```
(R-P386 第二批)
```

## `NR1L-PowerManagement-056`　`SWE-PM-013`　HMI interaction is disabled except for status changes

**Test Item**

```
All TLM, AMP, ICS, and DTV functionalities run in background and are ready but not HMI interaction is enabled within this status, except for the interaction that permit a change status.

(attempt an HMI interaction that changes the status -> The interaction that changes the status is accepted and the functionalities run in background)
```

**Pre-Conditions**

```
1. A LIN and CAN simulation tool is connected
2. Apply ENTER_PARTIAL_OPERATION
```

**Input Test Data**

```
NA
```

**Test procedure**

```
1. Attempt an HMI interaction that does not change the status
2. Attempt an HMI interaction that changes the status to check which one is accepted
```

**Expected Result**

```
1. The interaction that does not change the status is not accepted
2. The interaction that changes the status is accepted and the functionalities run in background
```

```
(R-P394 B5；規則 無)
```

## `NR1L-PowerManagement-057`　`SWE-PM-014`　Remote Start ends at ignition off: RemStartFail is set true

**Test Item**

```
IF TLM_Status.Info and $Telematic_Power$ == "Full-Operation" AND STATUS_BH_BCM2.RemStActvSts has a transition  from "Remote Start Active" to "Remote Start Not Active"THEN IF LTM_OperationalModeSts.Info is equal to "Ignition Pre Off" OR to "Ignition Off", TLM has to set RemStartFail = "True" THEN IF Phone_Call.Info == "Not Active", TLM has to set RemStartFail ="False" AND TLM_Status.Info and $Telematic_Power$ to "Standby" value and it passes to TLM Standby state.IF Phone_Call.Info == "Active" TLM has to set TLM_Status.Info and $Telematic_Power$ to "Timed" value and it passes to TLM Timed state.In this case, TLM has to stay in this state until Phone_Call.Info becomes equal to "Not_Active", OR at maximum until MaxCallTimeout expiration. See par. “Phone call management in Timed state” .ELSE IF LTM_OperationalModeSts.Info is not equal to "Ignition Pre Off" OR to "Ignition Off"  THEN TLM has to set RemStartFail = "False" AND TLM has to stay in the original state (Full Operation).IF TLM_Status.Info and $Telematic_Power$ == "Full-Operation"AND signal LTM_OperationalModeSts.Info has a transition to "Ignition Pre Off" OR to "Ignition Off" valueAND STATUS_BH_BCM2.RemStActvSts is equal to "Remote Start Not Active"AND RemStartFail == "False"THENaccording to a time setting selectable in the TLM menu it is possible to have two different behaviours

(read $STATUS_TELEMATIC.PowerSts_Telematic$ -> The TLM passes to Standby)
```

**Pre-Conditions**

```
1. A LIN and CAN simulation tool is connected
2. $STATUS_TELEMATIC.PowerSts_Telematic$ = 4 (Full_Operation)
3. $STATUS_BH_BCM2.RemStActvSts$ = 1 (Remote Start Active)
4. No phone call is in progress on the bench
```

**Input Test Data**

```
NA
```

**Test procedure**

```
1. Send the signal $STATUS_BH_BCM1.OperationalModeSts$ = 2 (Ignition_Off)
2. Send the signal $STATUS_BH_BCM2.RemStActvSts$ = 0 (Remote Start Not Active)
3. Read the signal $STATUS_TELEMATIC.PowerSts_Telematic$ and check that it is 1 (Standby)
```

**Expected Result**

```
1. The signal value $STATUS_BH_BCM1.OperationalModeSts$ = 2 (Ignition_Off) is received
2. The signal value $STATUS_BH_BCM2.RemStActvSts$ = 0 (Remote Start Not Active) is received
3. The signal value $STATUS_TELEMATIC.PowerSts_Telematic$ = 1 (Standby) is received
```

**Remarks**

```
(R-P376 丁案；原驗 RemStartFail 內部值，改驗其下游效果)
```

See par. “Phone call management in Timed state” .ELSE IF LTM_OperationalModeSts.Info is not equal to "Ignition Pre Off" OR to "Ignition Off"  THEN TLM has to set RemStartFail = "False" AND TLM has to stay in the original state (Full Operation).IF TLM_Status.Info and $Telematic_Power$ == "Full-Operation"AND signal LTM_OperationalModeSts.Info has a transition to "Ignition Pre Off" OR to "Ignition Off" valueAND STATUS_BH_BCM2.RemStActvSts is equal to "Remote Start Not Active"AND RemStartFail == "False"THENaccording to a time setting selectable in the TLM menu it is possible to have two different behaviours

## `NR1L-PowerManagement-066`　`SWE-PM-015`　Front_Panel_OnOff.Req press with no active call passes the TLM to Idle

**Test Item**

```
IF TLM_Status.Info and $Telematic_Power$ == "Full-Operation"AND signal Front_Panel_OnOff.Req has a transition from “Not_Pressed” value to “Pressed” valueTHEN IF Phone_Call.Info == Not_Active TLM has to set VPLastStatus to “OFF” value and to set TLM_Status.Info and $Telematic_Power$ to "Idle" value and then it passes to TLM Idle state.

(read VPLastStatus, TLM_Status.Info and $Telematic_Power$ -> VPLastStatus reads "OFF", TLM_Status.Info and $Telematic_Power$ read "Idle" and the TLM passes to TLM Idle state)
```

**Pre-Conditions**

```
1. A LIN and CAN simulation tool is connected
2. Apply ENTER_FULL_OPERATION
3. PENDING: DR-PW23 Phone_Call.Info
```

**Input Test Data**

```
NA
```

**Test procedure**

```
1. PENDING: DR-PW23 Front_Panel_OnOff.Req
2. PENDING: DR-PW23 VPLastStatus
3. Read the signal $STATUS_TELEMATIC.PowerSts_Telematic$ and check that it is the expected 4 (Full_Operation)
```

**Expected Result**

```
1. The TLM registers the press transition
2. VPLastStatus reads "OFF", TLM_Status.Info and $Telematic_Power$ read "Idle" and the TLM passes to TLM Idle state
3. VPLastStatus reads "OFF", TLM_Status.Info and $Telematic_Power$ read "Idle" and the TLM passes to TLM Idle state
```

**Remarks**

```
(R-P394 B5；規則 R4/R5) (R-P396 字典套用)
```

## `NR1L-PowerManagement-070`　`SWE-PM-016`　Rear camera activation keeps the TLM in Full-Operation

**Test Item**

```
IF TLM_Status.Info and $Telematic_Power$ == "Full-Operation"AND PROXI parameter Rear_View_Camera == "Present" AND Rear Camera becomes activeTHEN TLM has to stay in Full-Operation state and to manage rear view camera images on its screen, until Rear_Camera_Enable.Info passes to “False” again.

(read the TLM screen and Rear_Camera_Enable.Info -> The rear view camera images are managed on the TLM screen until Rear_Camera_Enable.Info passes to "False")
```

**Pre-Conditions**

```
1. A LIN and CAN simulation tool is connected
2. Apply ENTER_FULL_OPERATION
3. Rear_View_Camera reads "Present"
```

**Input Test Data**

```
NA
```

**Test procedure**

```
1. Make the Rear Camera become active
2. PENDING: DR-PW23 Rear_Camera_Enable.Info
```

**Expected Result**

```
1. The TLM stays in Full-Operation state on the camera activation
2. PENDING: DR-PW23 Rear_Camera_Enable.Info
```

**Remarks**

```
(R-P394 B5；規則 R4)
```

## `NR1L-PowerManagement-071`　`SWE-PM-017`　Rear camera deactivation restores the last active source

**Test Item**

```
IF TLM_Status.Info and $Telematic_Power$ == "Full-Operation"AND PROXI parameter Rear_View_Camera == "Present" AND Rear Camera becomes inactive,THENTLM has to manage the last active source, that depends also on Audio_Data_Exchange.Info value and on Phone_Call.Info values, that could have changed their value while showing rvc images, and on priorities of the sources.

(read the active source -> The last active source is managed according to Audio_Data_Exchange.Info and Phone_Call.Info values)
```

**Pre-Conditions**

```
1. A LIN and CAN simulation tool is connected
2. Apply ENTER_FULL_OPERATION
3. Rear_View_Camera reads "Present" and the Rear Camera is active
```

**Input Test Data**

```
NA
```

**Test procedure**

```
1. Make the Rear Camera become inactive
2. Read the source indicator and record its value, then check it against the recorded value
```

**Expected Result**

```
1. The TLM leaves the rear view camera images on the deactivation
2. PENDING: DR-PW23 Audio_Data_Exchange.Info
```

**Remarks**

```
(R-P394 B5；規則 R4) (R-P396 字典套用)
```

## `NR1L-PowerManagement-072`　`SWE-PM-018`　Ignition off in Idle passes the TLM to Standby

**Test Item**

```
IF TLM_Status.Info and $Telematic_Power$ == "Idle"AND signal LTM_OperationalModeSts has a transition to "Ignition Pre Off" OR to "Ignition Off" valueTHENTLM has to set TLM_Status.Info and $Telematic_Power$ to "Standby" value and then it passes to TLM Standby state.

(read TLM_Status.Info and $Telematic_Power$ -> TLM_Status.Info and $Telematic_Power$ read "Standby" and the TLM passes to TLM Standby state)
```

**Pre-Conditions**

```
1. A LIN and CAN simulation tool is connected
2. Apply ENTER_IDLE
```

**Input Test Data**

```
NA
```

**Test procedure**

```
1. Send the transition LTM_OperationalModeSts: "Ignition Off"
2. Read the signal $STATUS_TELEMATIC.PowerSts_Telematic$ and check that it is the expected 3 (Idle)
```

**Expected Result**

```
1. The TLM registers the transition without a bus error
2. TLM_Status.Info and $Telematic_Power$ read "Standby" and the TLM passes to TLM Standby state
```

**Remarks**

```
(R-P394 B5；規則 無) (R-P396 字典套用)
```

## `NR1L-PowerManagement-079`　`SWE-PM-020`　Call ending on the Phone Main Screen returns the TLM to Idle

**Test Item**

```
Then, IF Phone_Call.Info turns back to "Not_Active" when TLM_Display.GUI is in Phone Main Screen, TLM shall set TLM_Status.Info and $Telematic_Power$ to "Idle" again and then it passes to TLM Idle state.IF Phone_Call.Info turns back to "Not_Active" when another Screen is active on TLM_Display.GUI (due to user actions during the call), TLM shall stay in Full-Operation state.        Refer to TLM HMI document for further details about the screens visualization.

(read TLM_Status.Info and $Telematic_Power$ -> TLM_Status.Info and $Telematic_Power$ read "Idle" and the TLM passes to TLM Idle state)
```

**Pre-Conditions**

```
1. A LIN and CAN simulation tool is connected
2. TLM_Status.Info reads "Full-Operation" entered through a call
3. TLM_Display.GUI is in Phone Main Screen
```

**Input Test Data**

```
NA
```

**Test procedure**

```
1. PENDING: DR-PW23 Phone_Call.Info
2. Read the signal $STATUS_TELEMATIC.PowerSts_Telematic$ and check that it is the expected 4 (Full_Operation)
```

**Expected Result**

```
1. The TLM registers the transition without a bus error
2. TLM_Status.Info and $Telematic_Power$ read "Idle" and the TLM passes to TLM Idle state
```

**Remarks**

```
(R-P394 B5；規則 R4) (R-P396 字典套用)
```

```
IF TLM_Status.Info and $Telematic_Power$ == "Idle" AND PROXI parameter Rear_View_Camera == "Present" AND Rear_Camera_Enable.Info passes from "False" to "True"THENTLM stays in Idle state but allows its screen only to show the rear view camera video on its screen.Refer to VF551 for details about video availability requirements on TLM screen state.

(read TLM_Status.Info and the screen content -> The screen shows the rear view camera video and nothing else)
```

```
1. A LIN and CAN simulation tool is connected
2. Apply ENTER_IDLE
```

```
NA
```

```
2. Read the HU screen and check that the "Rear View Camera" video is shown on it
3. Read the signal $STATUS_TELEMATIC.PowerSts_Telematic$ and check that it is still 3 (Idle)
```

**Expected Result**

```
1. PENDING: DR-PW23 Rear_Camera_Enable.Info
2. The "Rear View Camera" video is shown on the HU screen
```

**Test Item**

```
IF TLM_Status.Info and $Telematic_Power$ == "Full-Operation” || “Idle”AND signal PowerModeSts_Telematic passes from “Standard_Power” to “Logistic_Mode_On”, then TLM has to set TLM_Status.Info to "Logistic Idle" and $Telematic_Power$ to “Logistic_On” and then it passes to Logistic Idle state.

(read TLM_Status.Info and $Telematic_Power$ -> TLM_Status.Info reads "Logistic Idle", $Telematic_Power$ reads "Logistic_On" and the TLM passes to Logistic Idle state)
```

```
2. Apply ENTER_FULL_OPERATION
```

```
NA
```

**Test procedure**

```
1. Send the transition PowerModeSts_Telematic: "Standard_Power" to "Logistic_Mode_On"
2. Read the signal $STATUS_TELEMATIC.PowerSts_Telematic$ and check that it is the expected 4 (Full_Operation)
```

**Expected Result**

```
1. The TLM registers the transition without a bus error
2. TLM_Status.Info reads "Logistic Idle", $Telematic_Power$ reads "Logistic_On" and the TLM passes to Logistic Idle state
```

```
(R-P394 B5；規則 無) (R-P396 字典套用)
```

## `NR1L-PowerManagement-083`　`SWE-PM-023`　Leaving Ignition Off in Timed passes the TLM to Full-Operation

**Test Item**

```
IF TLM_Status.Info and $Telematic_Power$ == "Timed"AND signal LTM_OperationalModeSts.Info has a transition from "Ignition Off" to another valueTHENTLM has to set VPLastStatus  to “On” value and to set TLM_Status.Info and $Telematic_Power$ to “Full-Operation” value and then it passes to TLM Full-Operation state.

(read VPLastStatus, TLM_Status.Info and $Telematic_Power$ -> VPLastStatus reads "On", TLM_Status.Info and $Telematic_Power$ read "Full-Operation" and the TLM passes to TLM Full-Operation state)
```

**Pre-Conditions**

```
1. A LIN and CAN simulation tool is connected
2. Apply ENTER_TIMED
```

**Input Test Data**

```
NA
```

**Test procedure**

```
1. Send the signal $STATUS_BH_BCM1.OperationalModeSts$ = a value other than 2 (Ignition_Off) (DR-PW26)
2. PENDING: DR-PW23 VPLastStatus
3. Read the signal $STATUS_TELEMATIC.PowerSts_Telematic$ and check that it is the expected 2 (Timed)
```

**Expected Result**

```
1. The TLM registers the transition without a bus error
2. VPLastStatus reads "On", TLM_Status.Info and $Telematic_Power$ read "Full-Operation" and the TLM passes to TLM Full-Operation state
3. VPLastStatus reads "On", TLM_Status.Info and $Telematic_Power$ read "Full-Operation" and the TLM passes to TLM Full-Operation state
```

**Remarks**

```
(R-P394 B5；規則 無) (R-P396 字典套用)
```

## `NR1L-PowerManagement-084`　`SWE-PM-024`　Remote Start not active on leaving Ignition Off clears RemStartFail

**Test Item**

```
IF TLM_Status.Info and $Telematic_Power$ == "Timed"AND STATUS_BH_BCM2.RemStActvSts == "Remote Start Not Active"AND signal LTM_OperationalModeSts.Info has a transition from "Ignition Off" to another valueTHENTLM has to set VPLastStatus  to “On” value and  RemStartFail to "False"AND TLM has to set TLM_Status.Info and $Telematic_Power$ to “Full-Operation” value and then it passes to TLM Full-Operation state.

(read VPLastStatus, RemStartFail and TLM_Status.Info -> VPLastStatus reads "On", RemStartFail reads "False" and the TLM passes to TLM Full-Operation state)
```

**Pre-Conditions**

```
1. A LIN and CAN simulation tool is connected
2. Apply ENTER_TIMED
3. STATUS_BH_BCM2.RemStActvSts reads "Remote Start Not Active"
```

**Input Test Data**

```
NA
```

**Test procedure**

```
1. Send the signal $STATUS_BH_BCM1.OperationalModeSts$ = a value other than 2 (Ignition_Off) (DR-PW26)
2. PENDING: DR-PW23 RemStartFail
```

**Expected Result**

```
1. The TLM registers the transition without a bus error
2. PENDING: DR-PW23 RemStartFail
```

**Remarks**

```
(R-P394 B5；規則 R4) (R-P396 字典套用)
```

## `NR1L-PowerManagement-085`　`SWE-PM-025`　Front_Panel_OnOff.Req press in Timed with an active call shows a popup

```
NA
```

**Test procedure**

```
1. PENDING: DR-PW23 Front_Panel_OnOff.Req
2. Apply FUNC_STATE_Timed and check its Display sub-item
```

**Expected Result**

```
1. The TLM registers the press transition
2. A popup asking whether to transfer the call is shown to the user
```

**Remarks**

```
(R-P394 B5；規則 R4/R5) (R-P396 字典套用)
```

```
1. The active functionality stops and no source stays active
```

## `NR1L-PowerManagement-100`　`SWE-PM-028`　Antitheft success with a zero timeout takes Timeout1 from PROXI

**Test Item**

```
IF SwitchOff_Timeout_Setting.Req == 00 min or ( If Auto_SwitchOn_Setting.Req =="Active ", when  Timeout1 == 00 MIN" for LTM High Radio): THENTLM has to set Timeout1 to the value specified by PROXI parameter “Switch_Off_Time” (for example 20 minutes if Switch_Off_Time == 20 minutes), only for this case,  restoring it to "00 minutes" at next Ignition On event.

(read Timeout1 and then trigger an Ignition On event -> Timeout1 reads "00 minutes" again at the next Ignition On event)
```

**Pre-Conditions**

```
1. A LIN and CAN simulation tool is connected
2. SwitchOff_Timeout_Setting.Req reads "00 min"
3. Switch_Off_Time reads 20 minutes
```

**Input Test Data**

```
NA
```

**Test procedure**

```
1. PENDING: DR-PW23 SwitchOff_Timeout_Setting.Req 之設定方法（設為 "00 min"）
2. Apply ENTER_TIMED and read the signal $STATUS_TELEMATIC.PowerSts_Telematic$ and check that it is 2 (Timed)
3. Hold for the PROXI Switch_Off_Time value, then read the signal $STATUS_TELEMATIC.PowerSts_Telematic$ and check that it is 1 (Standby)
4. PENDING: DR-PW23 Antitheft_Result.Info 之觀察方法（antitheft 成功）
```

**Expected Result**

```
1. PENDING: DR-PW23 SwitchOff_Timeout_Setting.Req
2. The signal value $STATUS_TELEMATIC.PowerSts_Telematic$ = 2 (Timed) is received
3. The signal value $STATUS_TELEMATIC.PowerSts_Telematic$ = 1 (Standby) is received after the PROXI Switch_Off_Time value has elapsed
4. PENDING: DR-PW23 Antitheft_Result.Info
```

**Remarks**

```
(R-P386 第一批)
```

## `NR1L-PowerManagement-101`　`SWE-PM-028`　Antitheft success on LTM High takes Timeout1 from PROXI

**Test Item**

```
IF SwitchOff_Timeout_Setting.Req == 00 min or ( If Auto_SwitchOn_Setting.Req =="Active ", when  Timeout1 == 00 MIN" for LTM High Radio): THENTLM has to set Timeout1 to the value specified by PROXI parameter “Switch_Off_Time” (for example 20 minutes if Switch_Off_Time == 20 minutes), only for this case,  restoring it to "00 minutes" at next Ignition On event.

(read Timeout1 and then trigger an Ignition On event -> Timeout1 reads "00 minutes" again at the next Ignition On event)
```

**Pre-Conditions**

```
1. A LIN and CAN simulation tool is connected
2. An LTM High Radio is present in the bench configuration
3. Auto_SwitchOn_Setting.Req reads "Active " and Timeout1 reads "00 MIN"
4. Switch_Off_Time reads 20 minutes
```

**Input Test Data**

```
NA
```

**Test procedure**

```
1. PENDING: DR-PW23 SwitchOff_Timeout_Setting.Req 之設定方法（設為 "00 min"）
2. Apply ENTER_TIMED and read the signal $STATUS_TELEMATIC.PowerSts_Telematic$ and check that it is 2 (Timed)
3. Hold for the PROXI Switch_Off_Time value, then read the signal $STATUS_TELEMATIC.PowerSts_Telematic$ and check that it is 1 (Standby)
4. PENDING: DR-PW23 Antitheft_Result.Info 之觀察方法（antitheft 成功）
```

**Expected Result**

```
1. PENDING: DR-PW23 SwitchOff_Timeout_Setting.Req
2. The signal value $STATUS_TELEMATIC.PowerSts_Telematic$ = 2 (Timed) is received
3. The signal value $STATUS_TELEMATIC.PowerSts_Telematic$ = 1 (Standby) is received after the PROXI Switch_Off_Time value has elapsed
4. PENDING: DR-PW23 Antitheft_Result.Info
```

**Remarks**

```
(R-P386 第一批)
```

## `NR1L-PowerManagement-118`　`SWE-PM-039`　An SNA operational mode is handled as an ignition off event

**Test Item**

```
IF TLM receives signal LTM_OperationalModeSts.Info equal to "SNA" value  THEN TLM has to behave as an Ignition Pre Off or Ignition Off event occurs, according to par. "TLM Operative state management".

(read the TLM state against the operative state management rules -> The TLM behaves as for an Ignition Pre Off or Ignition Off event)
```

**Pre-Conditions**

```
1. A LIN and CAN simulation tool is connected
2. The TLM is in an Ignition On working condition
```

**Input Test Data**

```
NA
```

**Test procedure**

```
1. Apply ENTER_FULL_OPERATION, send the signal $STATUS_BH_BCM1.OperationalModeSts$ = 2 (Ignition_Off) (DR-PW26), then read the signal $STATUS_TELEMATIC.PowerSts_Telematic$ and record the value as State_ignoff
2. Apply ENTER_FULL_OPERATION again
3. Send the signal $STATUS_BH_BCM1.OperationalModeSts$ = 15 (SNA) (DR-PW26), then read the signal $STATUS_TELEMATIC.PowerSts_Telematic$ and check that it is the same value as State_ignoff
```

**Expected Result**

```
1. State_ignoff is recorded
2. The HU is in Full-Operation again
3. The signal value $STATUS_TELEMATIC.PowerSts_Telematic$ after the SNA value is the same as State_ignoff
```

**Remarks**

```
(R-P386 第一批)
```

## `NR1L-PowerManagement-119`　`SWE-PM-039`　A zero switch off timeout loads Timeout1 from the PROXI value

**Test Item**

```
IF TLM_Status.Info was equal to "Full-Operation" AND SwitchOff_Timeout_Setting.Req was equal to "00 min" or (Auto_SwitchOn_Setting.Req == Active for LTM High Radio), then TLM has to set Timeout1 to the "Switch_Off_Time" PROXI value.

(read Timeout1 against the configured parameter -> Timeout1 reads the "Switch_Off_Time" PROXI value)
```

**Pre-Conditions**

```
1. A LIN and CAN simulation tool is connected
2. TLM_Status.Info was equal to "Full-Operation"
```

**Input Test Data**

```
NA
```

**Test procedure**

```
1. PENDING: DR-PW23 SwitchOff_Timeout_Setting.Req 之設定方法
2. Apply ENTER_FULL_OPERATION
3. Send the signal $STATUS_BH_BCM1.OperationalModeSts$ = 2 (Ignition_Off) (DR-PW26)
4. Read the signal $STATUS_TELEMATIC.PowerSts_Telematic$ and check that it is 2 (Timed), then hold for the PROXI Switch_Off_Time value and check that it is 1 (Standby)
```

**Expected Result**

```
1. PENDING: DR-PW23 SwitchOff_Timeout_Setting.Req
2. The HU is in Full-Operation
3. The signal value $STATUS_BH_BCM1.OperationalModeSts$ = 2 (Ignition_Off) is received
4. The signal value $STATUS_TELEMATIC.PowerSts_Telematic$ = 2 (Timed) is received, and $STATUS_TELEMATIC.PowerSts_Telematic$ = 1 (Standby) is received after the PROXI Switch_Off_Time value has elapsed
```

**Remarks**

```
(R-P386 第一批)
```

## `NR1L-PowerManagement-123`　`SWE-PM-041`　No TLM function is available in the TLM off with network on status

```
No TLM, FPDM, AMP, ICS, and DTV functionality is available.
```

```
1. A LIN and CAN simulation tool is connected
2. The TLM is in an Ignition Off working condition
```

```
NA
```

**Test procedure**

```
1. Apply ENTER_STANDBY
2. Read the signal $STATUS_TELEMATIC.PowerSts_Telematic$ and check that it is 1 (Standby)
3. Apply FUNC_STATE_STANDBY and check each of its sub-items
```

**Expected Result**

```
1. The HU is in Standby
2. The signal value $STATUS_TELEMATIC.PowerSts_Telematic$ = 1 (Standby) is received
3. FUNC_STATE_STANDBY holds:
   a. No audio source is playing on the HU speakers
   b. No amplifier output is present on the HU speakers
   c. The HU display is off, except for the HMI Antitheft Screens (CFTS009-4941457)
   d. PENDING: DR-PW27 BoosterOUT / antenna supply OFF 位準值
   e. A USB device inserted on the bench is not enumerated and the AUX input does not play on the HU speakers
```

```
(R-P386 第一批)
```

## `NR1L-PowerManagement-153`　`SWE-PM-055`　The special package drives the Klipsch Splash Screen on the 2025 model year

**Test Item**

```
The ETM shall use $VC_SpecialPKG_IC$ = [Tungsten (147)] to display the Klipsch Splash Screen if $VC_MODEL_YEAR$ =    [2025] and $VC_VEH_LINE$ = [DT].      The ETM shall use $SplashScreen_Type$ = [Klipsch (7)]  to display the Klipsch Splash Screen if $VC_MODEL_YEAR$ > [2025] and $VC_VEH_LINE$ = [DT].

(read the shown Splash Screen -> The Klipsch Splash Screen is displayed)
```

```
1. A LIN and CAN simulation tool is connected
2. The ETM carries VC_MODEL_YEAR equal to "2025"
3. The ETM carries VC_VEH_LINE equal to "DT"
4. (DR-PW28)
```

**Input Test Data**

```
NA
```

**Test procedure**

```
1. Send the value $VC_SpecialPKG_IC$: "Tungsten (147)"
2. Read the HU screen and check that the "Splash Screen" is shown on it
```

**Expected Result**

```
1. The ETM accepts the configuration value
2. The Klipsch "Splash Screen" is displayed
```

**Remarks**

```
(R-P357(b)：曾與 NR1L-PowerManagement-196 五欄逐字相同而 Req ID 不同（SWE-PM-055 / SWE-PM-102），二列皆保留不得合併；**71 包字典套用後二者之 Procedure／ER 已相異，不再成對** —— 互註改為歷史記載) (R-P394 B5；規則 無) (R-P396 字典套用)
```

```

2. Set VC_VEH_BRAND = a value other than Fiat (DR-PW28)

```

**Expected Result**

```

2. The brand configuration is applied
3. The "Fiat Latam Logo" is shown on the "Brand Logo Screen" in place of the "Vehicle Brand Logo"
```


## `NR1L-PowerManagement-157`　`SWE-PM-059`　A network sleep request in Standby passes the TLM to Sleep

**Test Item**

```
IF TLM_Status.Info and $Telematic_Power$ == "Standby"AND a Network Sleep request occursTHEN, provided that the boot of TLM has been completed, TLM has to set TLM_Status.Info and $Telematic_Power$ to “Sleep” value and then it has to pass to Sleep state AND Shutdown_Time starts.

(read the TLM state and the shutdown counter -> Shutdown_Time starts)
```

**Pre-Conditions**

```
1. A LIN and CAN simulation tool is connected
2. Apply ENTER_STANDBY
3. The boot of the TLM has been completed
```

**Input Test Data**

```
NA
```

**Test procedure**

```
1. Send the request A Network Sleep request
2. Read the signal $STATUS_TELEMATIC.PowerSts_Telematic$ and check that it is the expected 1 (Standby)
3. shutdown counter
```

**Expected Result**

```
1. TLM_Status.Info and $Telematic_Power$ read "Sleep" and the TLM passes to Sleep state
2. Shutdown_Time starts
3. Shutdown_Time starts
```

**Remarks**

```
(R-P394 B5；規則 無) (R-P396 字典套用)
```

## `NR1L-PowerManagement-158`　`SWE-PM-059`　A network sleep request during boot is served only after the boot ends

**Test Item**

```
If TLM Boot is not ended, TLM has to wait for its end before passing to Sleep state and starting the Shutdown_Time.

(read the TLM state and the shutdown counter at the end of the boot -> Shutdown_Time starts only after the end of the boot)
```

**Pre-Conditions**

```
1. A LIN and CAN simulation tool is connected
2. Apply ENTER_STANDBY
3. The boot of the TLM is not ended
```

**Input Test Data**

```
NA
```

**Test procedure**

```
1. Send the request A Network Sleep request
2. Read the TLM state and the shutdown counter at the end of the boot to check the wait
```

**Expected Result**

```
1. The TLM waits for the end of the boot before passing to Sleep state
2. Shutdown_Time starts only after the end of the boot
```

**Remarks**

```
(R-P394 B5；規則 無)
```

```
NA

```

```
2. Read the signal $STATUS_TELEMATIC.PowerSts_Telematic$ and check that it is the expected 3 (Idle)
3. Apply FUNC_STATE_Idle and check its Display sub-item
```

```
1. The HU transitions from IDLE to FULL OPERATION
2. The disclaimer screen is bypassed
3. The disclaimer screen is bypassed
```

```
(R-P394 B5；規則 無) (R-P396 字典套用)
```

## `NR1L-PowerManagement-169`　`SWE-PM-075`　The HU leaves Timed one minute after the FOTA pop-up is left untouched

**Test Item**

```
If the HU Transitions to Timed mode due to the condition described in CFTS009-1809, the HU shall transition to Standby mode after: -1 minute has passed without the user interacting with the pop-up -The FOTA pop up is dismissed -$ACCDlyAct$ transitions from active to inactive

(read the HU mode after the idle period -> The HU transitions to Standby mode after 1 minute has passed)
```

**Pre-Conditions**

```
1. A LIN and CAN simulation tool is connected
2. The HU is in Timed mode due to the condition described in CFTS009-1809
```

**Input Test Data**

```
NA
```

**Test procedure**

```
1. PENDING: DR-PW27 CFTS057 —— FOTA update available 之建立方法
2. Apply ENTER_TIMED
3. Read the HU screen and check that the "FOTA update available" pop-up is shown
4. Hold for 60000 ms with no user interaction, then read the HU screen and check that the "FOTA update available" pop-up is still shown on it
5. Read the signal $STATUS_TELEMATIC.PowerSts_Telematic$ and check that it is 1 (Standby)
```

**Expected Result**

```
1. PENDING: DR-PW27 CFTS057
2. The HU is in Timed
3. The "FOTA update available" pop-up is shown on the HU screen
4. The "FOTA update available" pop-up is still shown on the HU screen after 60000 ms with no user interaction
5. The signal value $STATUS_TELEMATIC.PowerSts_Telematic$ = 1 (Standby) is received
```

**Remarks**

```
(R-P386 第一批)
```

## `NR1L-PowerManagement-174`　`SWE-PM-093`　Closing the driver door in SLEEP MODE plays the start-up animation

**Test Item**

```
While HU is in SLEEP MODE, STANDBY MODE, or in PARTIAL OPERATION MODE and when driver door ajar status ($Door_Ajar_Status$) is changed to CLOSED, HU shall play a start-up animation as defined per HMI. The startup animation will be unique for each vehicle or/and brand as defined per PDO/HMI. If driver door is not present or removed for current vehicle ($DriverDoorOnOffSts$ = [DOOR_OFF]), or if HU changes mode (due to ignition event or to due to HU power mode status change to BODY ON or to TIMED MODE while driver door ajar status ($Door_Ajar_Status$) is OPEN, the HU shall skip start-up animation. While HU is playing a start-up animation and HU changes mode (due to ignition event or to HU power mode status changes to BODY ON or to TIMED MODE) or if an ignition crank event ($PowerMode$ = [IGN_START ]), then HU shall cancel current start-up animation and switch required power mode as defined. Once a start-up animation is played, HU shall not play the next start-up animation until the next CAN wakeup cycle OR at least 30 minutes passed from last time the start-up animation was played ;whichever is greater; given all other conditions are met for startup animation to play as defined here.

(read the screen -> The HU plays a start-up animation)
```

**Pre-Conditions**

```
1. A LIN and CAN simulation tool is connected
2. Apply ENTER_SLEEP
3. A driver door is present for the vehicle
4. (DR-PW28)
```

**Input Test Data**

```
NA
```

**Test procedure**

```
1. Send the change Door_Ajar_Status: changed to CLOSED
2. Apply FUNC_STATE_Sleep and check its Display sub-item
```

**Expected Result**

```
1. The HU registers the change without a bus error
2. The HU plays a "Start-up Animation"
```

**Remarks**

```
(R-P394 B5；規則 無) (R-P396 字典套用)
```

## `NR1L-PowerManagement-177`　`SWE-PM-093`　A removed driver door makes the HU skip the start-up animation

**Test Item**

```
While HU is in SLEEP MODE, STANDBY MODE, or in PARTIAL OPERATION MODE and when driver door ajar status ($Door_Ajar_Status$) is changed to CLOSED, HU shall play a start-up animation as defined per HMI. The startup animation will be unique for each vehicle or/and brand as defined per PDO/HMI. If driver door is not present or removed for current vehicle ($DriverDoorOnOffSts$ = [DOOR_OFF]), or if HU changes mode (due to ignition event or to due to HU power mode status change to BODY ON or to TIMED MODE while driver door ajar status ($Door_Ajar_Status$) is OPEN, the HU shall skip start-up animation. While HU is playing a start-up animation and HU changes mode (due to ignition event or to HU power mode status changes to BODY ON or to TIMED MODE) or if an ignition crank event ($PowerMode$ = [IGN_START ]), then HU shall cancel current start-up animation and switch required power mode as defined. Once a start-up animation is played, HU shall not play the next start-up animation until the next CAN wakeup cycle OR at least 30 minutes passed from last time the start-up animation was played ;whichever is greater; given all other conditions are met for startup animation to play as defined here.

(read the screen -> The HU skips the start-up animation)
```

**Pre-Conditions**

```
1. A LIN and CAN simulation tool is connected
2. Apply ENTER_STANDBY
```

**Input Test Data**

```
NA
```

**Test procedure**

```
1. Send the value $DriverDoorOnOffSts$: "DOOR_OFF" and close the driver door
2. Apply FUNC_STATE_Standby and check its Display sub-item
```

**Expected Result**

```
1. The HU registers the value without a bus error
2. The HU skips the "Start-up Animation"
```

**Remarks**

```
(R-P394 B5；規則 無) (R-P396 字典套用)
```

## `NR1L-PowerManagement-182`　`SWE-PM-093`　A second start-up animation waits for the wakeup cycle or thirty minutes

**Test Item**

```
While HU is in SLEEP MODE, STANDBY MODE, or in PARTIAL OPERATION MODE and when driver door ajar status ($Door_Ajar_Status$) is changed to CLOSED, HU shall play a start-up animation as defined per HMI. The startup animation will be unique for each vehicle or/and brand as defined per PDO/HMI. If driver door is not present or removed for current vehicle ($DriverDoorOnOffSts$ = [DOOR_OFF]), or if HU changes mode (due to ignition event or to due to HU power mode status change to BODY ON or to TIMED MODE while driver door ajar status ($Door_Ajar_Status$) is OPEN, the HU shall skip start-up animation. While HU is playing a start-up animation and HU changes mode (due to ignition event or to HU power mode status changes to BODY ON or to TIMED MODE) or if an ignition crank event ($PowerMode$ = [IGN_START ]), then HU shall cancel current start-up animation and switch required power mode as defined. Once a start-up animation is played, HU shall not play the next start-up animation until the next CAN wakeup cycle OR at least 30 minutes passed from last time the start-up animation was played ;whichever is greater; given all other conditions are met for startup animation to play as defined here.

(read the screen against the elapsed time -> A start-up animation plays again only at the next CAN wakeup cycle or after 30 minutes, whichever is greater)
```

**Pre-Conditions**

```
1. A LIN and CAN simulation tool is connected
2. The HU has just played a start-up animation
3. All other conditions for the animation hold
```

**Input Test Data**

```
NA
```

**Test procedure**

```
1. Set Door_Ajar_Status = "Open" then "Closed" again within the same CAN wakeup cycle (DR-PW28)
2. Read the HU screen and check that the "Start-up Animation" is not played on it
3. Hold for 1800000 ms, repeat the door event, then read the HU screen and check that the "Start-up Animation" is played on it
```

**Expected Result**

```
1. The door event is registered within the same CAN wakeup cycle
2. The "Start-up Animation" is not played on the HU screen
3. After 1800000 ms the "Start-up Animation" is played on the HU screen
```

**Remarks**

```
(R-P386 第二批)
```

## `NR1L-PowerManagement-194`　`SWE-PM-101`　SDARS present without audio brand adds the Sirius logo

**Test Item**

```
- IF SDARS_Presence == "Present" AND Audio_Brand == "No Audio Brand"THEN TLM has to show the Sirius logo in addition to the vehicle brand logo;

(read the shown logos -> The Sirius logo is shown in addition to the vehicle brand logo)
```

**Pre-Conditions**

```
1. A LIN and CAN simulation tool is connected
2. SDARS_Presence reads "Present"
```

**Input Test Data**

```
NA
```

**Test procedure**

```
1. Set VC_VEH_BRAND = the brand under test (DR-PW28)
2. Apply ENTER_FULL_OPERATION and let the brand logo screen be shown
3. Read the "Brand Logo Screen" and check which of the named logos is shown on it
```

**Expected Result**

```
1. The brand configuration is applied
2. The "Brand Logo Screen" is shown
3. The logo shown on the "Brand Logo Screen" is the one named for this configuration
```

**Remarks**

```
(R-P386 第一批) (R-P357(b)：與 NR1L-PowerManagement-151 五欄逐字相同而 Req ID 不同（SWE-PM-054 / SWE-PM-101），二列皆保留不得合併；互註對方 tc_id)
```
