## `NR1L-PowerManagement-196`　`SWE-PM-102`　The special package drives the Klipsch Splash Screen on the 2025 model year

**Test Item**

```
The ETM shall use $VC_SpecialPKG_IC$ = [Tungsten (147)] to display the Klipsch Splash Screen if $VC_MODEL_YEAR$ =    [2025] and $VC_VEH_LINE$ = [DT].      The ETM shall use $SplashScreen_Type$ = [Klipsch (7)]  to display the Klipsch Splash Screen if $VC_MODEL_YEAR$ > [2025] and $VC_VEH_LINE$ = [DT].

(read the shown Splash Screen -> The Klipsch Splash Screen is displayed)
```

**Pre-Conditions**

```
1. A LIN and CAN simulation tool is connected
2. The ETM carries VC_MODEL_YEAR equal to "2025"
3. The ETM carries VC_VEH_LINE equal to "DT"
(DR-PW28)
```

**Input Test Data**

```
NA
```

**Test procedure**

```
1. Send the value $VC_SpecialPKG_IC$: "Tungsten (147)"
2. Read the HU screen and check that the "Splash Screen" is shown on it
```

**Expected Result**

```
1. The ETM accepts the configuration value
2. The Klipsch "Splash Screen" is displayed
```

**Remarks**

```
(R-P357(b)：曾與 NR1L-PowerManagement-153 五欄逐字相同而 Req ID 不同（SWE-PM-055 / SWE-PM-102），二列皆保留不得合併；**71 包字典套用後二者之 Procedure／ER 已相異，不再成對** —— 互註改為歷史記載) (R-P394 B5；規則 R3/R6) (R-P396 字典套用)
```

## `NR1L-PowerManagement-198`　`SWE-PM-103`　Audio is off and only the Splash Screen is allowed in this status

**Test Item**

```
This status is related to TLM audio is OFF. TLM shall allow only Splash Screen visualization on its display.  ICS functionalities are available.  DTV shall be OFF.

(read the audio path and the display -> The TLM allows only Splash Screen visualization on its display)
```

**Pre-Conditions**

```
1. A LIN and CAN simulation tool is connected
2. The TLM is in an Ignition On working condition
```

**Input Test Data**

```
NA
```

**Test procedure**

```
1. Bring the TLM to the status related to TLM audio is OFF
2. Read the HU speakers and check whether entertainment audio output is present on them
3. Apply FUNC_STATE_<STATE> and check its Display sub-item
```

**Expected Result**

```
1. The TLM audio is OFF
2. The TLM allows only "Splash Screen" visualization on its display
3. The TLM allows only "Splash Screen" visualization on its display
```

**Remarks**

```
(R-P394 B5；規則 無) (R-P396 字典套用) (站④-2：`<STATE>` 未能由本條推定，待分析層指定)
```

## `NR1L-PowerManagement-202`　`SWE-PM-103`　ICS stays available while DTV is off in this status

**Test Item**

```
This status is related to TLM audio is OFF. TLM shall allow only Splash Screen visualization on its display.  ICS functionalities are available.  DTV shall be OFF.

(read the ICS functions and the DTV -> DTV is OFF)
```

**Pre-Conditions**

```
1. A LIN and CAN simulation tool is connected
2. The TLM is in an Ignition On Engine On working condition
```

**Input Test Data**

```
NA
```

**Test procedure**

```
1. Apply ENTER_IDLE
2. Read the signal $STATUS_TELEMATIC.PowerSts_Telematic$ and check that it is 3 (Idle)
3. Read the HU screen and check that only the "Splash Screen" is shown on it and no audio source is playing on the HU speakers
4. Touch the screen and read the bus trace, and check that $TELEMATIC_FD_5.CM_TCH_STAT$ is 1 (TCH_PSD) and that $TELEMATIC_FD_5.CM_TCH_X_COORD$ and $TELEMATIC_FD_5.CM_TCH_Y_COORD$ carry the touch coordinates
```

**Expected Result**

```
1. The HU is in Idle
2. The signal value $STATUS_TELEMATIC.PowerSts_Telematic$ = 3 (Idle) is received
3. Only the "Splash Screen" is shown on the HU screen and no audio source is playing on the HU speakers
4. The signal value $TELEMATIC_FD_5.CM_TCH_STAT$ = 1 (TCH_PSD) is received together with the touch coordinates on $TELEMATIC_FD_5.CM_TCH_X_COORD$ and $TELEMATIC_FD_5.CM_TCH_Y_COORD$
```

**Remarks**

```
(R-P386 第二批)
```

## `NR1L-PowerManagement-203`　`SWE-PM-104`　The splash and disclaimer screens appear on the first transition to Timed

**Test Item**

```
The splash screen and disclaimer screen shall be shown the first time each bus cycle the HU transitions to Timed or Full Operation modes.

(read the screen sequence -> The disclaimer screen is shown)
```

**Pre-Conditions**

```
1. A LIN and CAN simulation tool is connected
2. A new bus cycle has started
3. Neither screen has been shown in this bus cycle
```

**Input Test Data**

```
NA
```

**Test procedure**

```
1. Bring the HU to Timed mode for the first time in the bus cycle
2. Read the HU screen and check that the "Start-up Animation", the "Splash Screen" and the "Disclaimer" are shown separately in that order
```

**Expected Result**

```
1. The "Splash Screen" is shown
2. The disclaimer screen is shown
```

**Remarks**

```
(R-P394 B5；規則 無) (R-P396 字典套用)
```

```

(read the screen -> The disclaimer screen is shown)
```

**Pre-Conditions**

```
1. A LIN and CAN simulation tool is connected
2. Apply ENTER_IDLE
3. The disclaimer needs to be shown
```

**Input Test Data**

```
NA
```

**Test procedure**

```
1. Bring the HU from Idle to Timed mode for the first time in the bus cycle
2. Apply FUNC_STATE_Idle and check its Display sub-item
```

**Expected Result**

```
1. The HU reaches Timed mode
2. The disclaimer screen is shown
```

**Remarks**

```
(R-P394 B5；規則 R1) (R-P396 字典套用)
```

## `NR1L-PowerManagement-208`　`SWE-PM-105`　An ongoing call temporarily skips the disclaimer and splash screens

**Test Item**

```
The disclaimer and splash screen can be temporarily skipped for incoming/outgoing/ongoing calls, climate pop-ups, backup camera, SOS and Assist calls, and FOTA pop ups, but must be displayed at the next transition to Timed or Full Operation modes during that bus cycle. See HMI logic and Flow "Startup" requirements for details.

(read the screen -> The disclaimer and splash screen are temporarily skipped)
```

**Pre-Conditions**

```
1. A LIN and CAN simulation tool is connected
2. A new bus cycle has started
```

**Input Test Data**

```
NA
```

**Test procedure**

```
1. Bring the HU to Timed mode while the event An ongoing call at the moment of the transition holds
2. Apply FUNC_STATE_Timed and check its Display sub-item
```

**Expected Result**

```
1. The HU reaches Timed mode
2. The disclaimer and "Splash Screen" are temporarily skipped
```

**Remarks**

```
(R-P394 B5；規則 R3) (R-P396 字典套用)
```

```
For all variations of the disclaimer screen and geolocation pop up listed below, if the configuration parameter $Ecall_Button_Variant$ = [SOS] the HU shall use the SOS text for the disclaimer.

(read the disclaimer wording -> The HU uses the SOS text for the disclaimer)
```

**Pre-Conditions**

```
1. A LIN and CAN simulation tool is connected
2. Set VC_VEH_BRAND = a value other than "Maserati" (DR-PW28)
3. Set TBM_Present = "Present" (DR-PW28)
4. PROXI Ecall_Button_Variant = the variant for this market
```

**Input Test Data**

```
NA
```

**Test procedure**

```
1. Apply ENTER_FULL_OPERATION and let the HU reach the disclaimer presentation
2. Read the "Disclaimer" screen text and check that it contains "SOS"
```

**Expected Result**

```
1. The HU reaches the disclaimer presentation
2. The "Disclaimer" screen text contains "SOS"
```

**Remarks**

```
(R-P386 第一批)
```

## `NR1L-PowerManagement-218`　`SWE-PM-108`　A non Maserati brand shows the core disclaimer once every thirty ignition cycles

**Test Item**

```
If $VC_VEH_BRAND$ <> [Maserati] the R1 Head Unit shall only show the core disclaimer screen once every 30 ignition cycles

(read the screen across the cycles -> The core disclaimer screen is shown only once every 30 ignition cycles)
```

**Pre-Conditions**

```
1. A LIN and CAN simulation tool is connected
2. $VC_VEH_BRAND$ reads a value other than "Maserati"
```

**Input Test Data**

```
Ignition cycles: 31 consecutive Ignition Off -> Ignition On cycles
```

**Test procedure**

```
1. Repeat the ignition cycle listed in Input Test Data 31 times, sending $STATUS_BH_BCM1.OperationalModeSts$ = 2 (Ignition_Off) then = 4 (Ignition_On) (DR-PW26)
2. After each cycle, read the HU screen and record whether the "Disclaimer" screen is shown
3. Count the cycles in which the "Disclaimer" screen was shown
```

**Expected Result**

```
1. All 31 ignition cycles are registered without a bus error
2. The presence of the "Disclaimer" screen is recorded for each of the 31 cycles
3. The "Disclaimer" screen was shown in exactly 2 cycles: cycle 1 and cycle 31
```

**Remarks**

```
(R-P386 第一批)
```

(DR-PW28)

NA

2. Read the HU screen and check that the "Geolocation + SOS" pop-up is shown on it  —— PENDING: DR-PW27 GDPR flow in HMI
```

**Expected Result**

```
1. The HU reaches the startup presentation
2. The HU follows the GDPR Non-Maserati startup flow in the HMI

(R-P394 B5；規則 R6) (R-P396 字典套用)

## `NR1L-PowerManagement-225`　`SWE-PM-114`　An incoming call from IDLE bypasses the not yet shown disclaimer screen

**Test Item**

```
IF the HU transitions from IDLE to FULL OPERATION due to an incoming phone call, THEN the HU shall bypass the disclaimer screen if it has not yet been shown.

(read the HU mode and the screen -> The disclaimer screen is bypassed)
```

**Pre-Conditions**

```
1. A LIN and CAN simulation tool is connected
2. Apply ENTER_IDLE
3. The disclaimer screen has not yet been shown
```

**Input Test Data**

```
NA
```

**Test procedure**

```
1. Let the bench place the call An incoming phone call
2. Read the signal $STATUS_TELEMATIC.PowerSts_Telematic$ and check that it is the expected 3 (Idle)
3. Apply FUNC_STATE_Idle and check its Display sub-item
```

**Expected Result**

```
1. The HU transitions from IDLE to FULL OPERATION
2. The disclaimer screen is bypassed
3. The disclaimer screen is bypassed
```

**Remarks**

```
(R-P357(b)：與 NR1L-PowerManagement-162 五欄逐字相同而 Req ID 不同（SWE-PM-068 / SWE-PM-114），二列皆保留不得合併；互註對方 tc_id) (R-P394 B5；規則 R1/R3) (R-P396 字典套用)
```

## `NR1L-PowerManagement-226`　`SWE-PM-115`　The disclaimer bypassed for a call is shown at the next FULL OPERATION

**Test Item**

```
IF the HU transitions from IDLE to FULL OPERATION due to an incoming phone call, the HU shall bypass the disclaimer.  THEN if the HU returns to IDLE when the phone call becomes inactive, the HU shall show the disclaimer at the next transition to FULL OPERATION.

(bring the HU to FULL OPERATION again and read the screen -> The disclaimer is shown at the next transition to FULL OPERATION)
```

**Pre-Conditions**

```
1. A LIN and CAN simulation tool is connected
2. Apply ENTER_IDLE
3. The disclaimer has not yet been shown
```

**Input Test Data**

```
NA
```

**Test procedure**

```
1. Let the bench place and then end the call An incoming phone call that then becomes inactive
2. Bring the HU to FULL OPERATION again and read the screen to check the disclaimer
```

**Expected Result**

```
1. The HU bypasses the disclaimer for the call and returns to IDLE
2. The disclaimer is shown at the next transition to FULL OPERATION
```

**Remarks**

```
(R-P357(b)：與 NR1L-PowerManagement-165 五欄逐字相同而 Req ID 不同（SWE-PM-070 / SWE-PM-115），二列皆保留不得合併；互註對方 tc_id) (R-P394 B5；規則 R1/R3)
```

## `NR1L-PowerManagement-231`　`SWE-PM-080`　The theme special package value is sent while the CAN network is awake

**Test Item**

```
When the CAN network is awake, the HU shall send the special package value associated with that theme in $Radio_Theme$. if the theme has changed, the HU will update and send the new $Radio_Theme$ within <Tsend> See the $VC_SpecialPKG$ column of the [PDO Theme Configuration] reference document for the value to send in $Radio_Theme$.

(read $Radio_Theme$ against the applied theme -> The value sent in $Radio_Theme$ is the special package value associated with that theme)
```

**Pre-Conditions**

```
1. A LIN and CAN simulation tool is connected
2. The CAN network is awake
3. A theme is applied on the HU
```

**Input Test Data**

```
NA
```

**Test procedure**

```
1. Observe the bus traffic while the CAN network stays awake
2. Read the signal $RADIO_B4.Radio_Theme$ and check its value
3. PENDING: DR-PW27 [PDO Theme Configuration]
```

**Expected Result**

```
1. The HU sends $Radio_Theme$ on the bus
2. The value sent in $Radio_Theme$ is the special package value associated with that theme
3. The value sent in $Radio_Theme$ is the special package value associated with that theme
```

**Remarks**

```
(R-P357(b)：與 NR1L-PowerManagement-246 五欄逐字相同而 Req ID 不同（SWE-PM-080 / SWE-PM-086），二列皆保留不得合併；互註對方 tc_id) (R-P394 B5；規則 無) (R-P396 字典套用)
```

## `NR1L-PowerManagement-233`　`SWE-PM-081`　The Chrysler brand selects the Chrysler font

**Test Item**

```
The HU shall use $VC_VEH_BRAND$ to determine the correct font to display. Valid values are as follows:$VC_VEH_BRAND$  = [Chrysler] shall indicate Chrysler font. $VC_VEH_BRAND$  = [Dodge] shall indicate Dodge font. $VC_VEH_BRAND$  = [Jeep] shall indicate Jeep font. $VC_VEH_BRAND$  = [Alpha Romeo] shall indicate Alfa Romeo font. $VC_VEH_BRAND$  = [Fiat] shall indicate Fiat font. (DEFAULT)$VC_VEH_BRAND$  = [Lancia] shall indicate Lancia font. $VC_VEH_BRAND$  = [Maserati] shall indicate Maserati font. $VC_VEH_BRAND$  = [Ram] shall indicate Ram font.$VC_VEH_BRAND$  = [Abarth] shall indicate Abarth font.$VC_VEH_BRAND$  = [Opel] shall indicate Opel font.$VC_VEH_BRAND$  = [Vauxhall] shall indicate Vauxhall font.$VC_VEH_BRAND$  = [Citroen] shall indicate Citroen font.$VC_VEH_BRAND$  = [Peugeot] shall indicate Peugeot font.

(read the displayed font -> The HU displays the Chrysler font)
```

**Pre-Conditions**

```
1. A LIN and CAN simulation tool is connected
2. The HU is displaying branded text
```

**Input Test Data**

```
NA
```

**Test procedure**

```
1. Set VC_VEH_BRAND = "Chrysler" (DR-PW28)
2. Read the branded text on the HU screen and check that it is rendered in the "Chrysler font"
```

**Expected Result**

```
1. The brand configuration is applied
2. The branded text is rendered in the "Chrysler font"
```

**Remarks**

```
(R-P386 第二批)
```

The HU shall use $VC_VEH_BRAND$ to determine the correct font to display. Valid values are as follows:$VC_VEH_BRAND$  = [Chrysler] shall indicate Chrysler font. $VC_VEH_BRAND$  = [Dodge] shall indicate Dodge font. $VC_VEH_BRAND$  = [Jeep] shall indicate Jeep font. $VC_VEH_BRAND$  = [Alpha Romeo] shall indicate Alfa Romeo font. $VC_VEH_BRAND$  = [Fiat] shall indicate Fiat font. (DEFAULT)$VC_VEH_BRAND$  = [Lancia] shall indicate Lancia font. $VC_VEH_BRAND$  = [Maserati] shall indicate Maserati font. $VC_VEH_BRAND$  = [Ram] shall indicate Ram font.$VC_VEH_BRAND$  = [Abarth] shall indicate Abarth font.$VC_VEH_BRAND$  = [Opel] shall indicate Opel font.$VC_VEH_BRAND$  = [Vauxhall] shall indicate Vauxhall font.$VC_VEH_BRAND$  = [Citroen] shall indicate Citroen font.$VC_VEH_BRAND$  = [Peugeot] shall indicate Peugeot font.

(read the displayed font -> The HU displays the Fiat font that the specification marks as DEFAULT)
```

**Pre-Conditions**

```
1. A LIN and CAN simulation tool is connected
2. The HU is displaying branded text
```

**Input Test Data**

```
NA
```

**Test procedure**

```
1. Set VC_VEH_BRAND = "Fiat" (DR-PW28)
2. Read the branded text on the HU screen and check that it is rendered in the "Fiat font"
```

**Expected Result**

```
1. The brand configuration is applied
2. The branded text is rendered in the "Fiat font"
```

**Remarks**

```
(R-P386 第二批)
```

## `NR1L-PowerManagement-242`　`SWE-PM-084`　The recirc icon follows the PROXI parameters on the Atlantis architecture

**Test Item**

```
CUSW/AtlLo/AtlMi/AtlHi:The HU shall use $VC_VEH_LINE$ and the$Car_Shape_Configuration$ and $Number_of_Doors$ PROXI parameters to determine the correct recirc icon to display. See HMI release and PDO graphics files for ICON assignments.PNET: The HU shall use the $VC_VEH_LINE$ and the $VC_BODY_STYLE$ signals to determine the correct recirc icon to display. See HMI release and PDO graphics files for ICON assignments

(read the shown recirc icon -> The recirc icon matches the assignment for that vehicle line and car shape)
```

**Pre-Conditions**

```
1. A LIN and CAN simulation tool is connected
2. The HU runs the CUSW or Atlantis architecture
3. The climate screen showing the recirc icon is reachable
```

**Input Test Data**

```
NA
```

**Test procedure**

```
1. Apply the configuration: PROXI Car_Shape_Configuration and PROXI Number_of_Doors
2. Read the climate screen and check which "Recirc Icon" is shown on it
3. PENDING: DR-PW27 HMI release —— 該組態所對應之 recirc icon 指派
```

**Expected Result**

```
1. The configuration is applied
2. The "Recirc Icon" shown on the climate screen is read
3. PENDING: DR-PW27 HMI release
```

**Remarks**

```
(R-P386 第二批)
```

## `NR1L-PowerManagement-246`　`SWE-PM-086`　The theme special package value is sent on this chapter while the network is awake

**Test Item**

```
When the CAN network is awake, the HU shall send the special package value associated with that theme in $Radio_Theme$. if the theme has changed, the HU will update and send the new $Radio_Theme$ within <Tsend> See the $VC_SpecialPKG$ column of the [PDO Theme Configuration] reference document for the value to send in $Radio_Theme$.

(read $Radio_Theme$ against the applied theme -> The value sent in $Radio_Theme$ is the special package value associated with that theme)
```

**Pre-Conditions**

```
1. A LIN and CAN simulation tool is connected
2. The CAN network is awake
3. A theme is applied on the HU
```

**Input Test Data**

```
NA
```

**Test procedure**

```
1. Observe the bus traffic while the CAN network stays awake
2. Read the signal $RADIO_B4.Radio_Theme$ and check its value
3. PENDING: DR-PW27 [PDO Theme Configuration]
```

**Expected Result**

```
1. The HU sends $Radio_Theme$ on the bus
2. The value sent in $Radio_Theme$ is the special package value associated with that theme
3. The value sent in $Radio_Theme$ is the special package value associated with that theme
```

**Remarks**

```
(R-P357(b)：與 NR1L-PowerManagement-231 五欄逐字相同而 Req ID 不同（SWE-PM-080 / SWE-PM-086），二列皆保留不得合併；互註對方 tc_id) (R-P394 B5；規則 無) (R-P396 字典套用)
```

## `NR1L-PowerManagement-247`　`SWE-PM-086`　A theme change on this chapter updates the sent value within the send window

**Test Item**

```
When the CAN network is awake, the HU shall send the special package value associated with that theme in $Radio_Theme$. if the theme has changed, the HU will update and send the new $Radio_Theme$ within <Tsend> See the $VC_SpecialPKG$ column of the [PDO Theme Configuration] reference document for the value to send in $Radio_Theme$.

(read $Radio_Theme$ and its timing -> The new value is sent within Tsend of the theme change)
```

**Pre-Conditions**

```
1. A LIN and CAN simulation tool is connected
2. The CAN network is awake
3. A theme is applied on the HU
(DR-PW28)
```

**Input Test Data**

```
NA
```

**Test procedure**

```
1. Send the value VC_SpecialPKG: a second value mapped to a different theme
2. Read the signal $RADIO_B4.Radio_Theme$ and check its value
3. PENDING: DR-PW27 <Tsend> 之值
```

**Expected Result**

```
1. The HU sends the new $Radio_Theme$ value
2. The new value is sent within Tsend of the theme change
3. The new value is sent within Tsend of the theme change
```

**Remarks**

```
(R-P357(b)：與 NR1L-PowerManagement-232 五欄逐字相同而 Req ID 不同（SWE-PM-080 / SWE-PM-086），二列皆保留不得合併；互註對方 tc_id) (R-P394 B5；規則 R3/R6) (R-P396 字典套用)
```

## `NR1L-PowerManagement-261`　`SWE-PM-001`　Full-Operation keeps the TLM ON with all functionalities available

**Test Item**

```
All TLM, AMP/ICS/DTV functionalities are available.

(read the TLM, AMP, ICS and DTV functionality availability -> All TLM, AMP, ICS and DTV functionalities are available)
```

**Pre-Conditions**

```
1. A LIN and CAN simulation tool is connected
2. The ignition working condition is Ignition On
3. Apply ENTER_FULL_OPERATION
```

**Input Test Data**

```
NA
```

**Test procedure**

```
1. Read the signal $STATUS_TELEMATIC.PowerSts_Telematic$ and check that it is the expected 4 (Full_Operation)
2. Apply FUNC_STATE_Full_Operation and check all of its sub-items
3. Read the HU speakers and check whether entertainment audio output is present on them
4. Touch the screen and read the bus trace, and check whether $TELEMATIC_FD_5.CM_TCH_STAT$ is 1 (TCH_PSD) with coordinates
5. Apply FUNC_STATE_Full_Operation and check its Display sub-item  (DTV 影像僅經顯示可見；規格無獨立 DTV 觀察面)
```

**Expected Result**

```
1. The TLM is ON
2. All TLM, AMP, ICS and DTV functionalities are available
3. All TLM, AMP, ICS and DTV functionalities are available
4. All TLM, AMP, ICS and DTV functionalities are available
5. All TLM, AMP, ICS and DTV functionalities are available
```

**Remarks**

```
(R-P394 B5；規則 R1) (R-P396 字典套用)
```

## `NR1L-PowerManagement-262`　`SWE-PM-001`　Full-Operation holds in each listed ignition working condition

**Test Item**

```
All TLM, AMP/ICS/DTV functionalities are available.

(read TLM_Status.Info after each one -> The TLM stays ON with all TLM, AMP, ICS and DTV functionalities available in each of the listed conditions)
```

**Pre-Conditions**

```
1. A LIN and CAN simulation tool is connected
The TLM is in Full-Operation
```

**Input Test Data**

```
Ignition working conditions: Ignition Pre_Start, Ignition Start, Ignition Cranking, Ignition On Engine On
```

**Test procedure**

```
1. Apply each ignition working condition listed in Input Test Data in turn by sending $STATUS_BH_BCM1.OperationalModeSts$ (DR-PW26)
2. After each one, read the signal $STATUS_TELEMATIC.PowerSts_Telematic$ and check that it is 4 (Full_Operation)
3. After each one, check that the audio active source keeps playing on the HU speakers
```

**Expected Result**

```
1. Each listed ignition working condition is registered without a bus error
2. The signal value $STATUS_TELEMATIC.PowerSts_Telematic$ = 4 (Full_Operation) is received after each one
3. The audio active source keeps playing on the HU speakers after each one
```

**Remarks**

```
(R-P383)
```

4. $Telematic_Power$ reads "Partial_Operation" and the AMP, ICS and DTV are OFF

## `NR1L-PowerManagement-271`　`SWE-PM-004`　Timed keeps the TLM on with all functionalities available for a limited time

**Test Item**

```
TLM AMP/ICS/DTV functionalities are available only for a limited time.

(read the TLM state again after Timeout1 has elapsed -> The availability is limited in time and does not continue past Timeout1)
```

**Pre-Conditions**

```
1. A LIN and CAN simulation tool is connected
2. $STATUS_BH_BCM1.OperationalModeSts$ = 2 (Ignition_Off) (DR-PW26)
3. Apply ENTER_TIMED
```

**Input Test Data**

```
NA
```

**Test procedure**

```
1. Read the signal $STATUS_TELEMATIC.PowerSts_Telematic$ and check that it is 2 (Timed), and check that the audio active source keeps playing on the HU speakers
2. Hold for the $BCM_FD_27.Comfort_Enable_Time$ value with no phone call active
3. Read the signal $STATUS_TELEMATIC.PowerSts_Telematic$ and check that it is 1 (Standby)
```

**Expected Result**

```
1. The signal value $STATUS_TELEMATIC.PowerSts_Telematic$ = 2 (Timed) is received and the audio active source keeps playing on the HU speakers
2. The $BCM_FD_27.Comfort_Enable_Time$ value elapses with no phone call active
3. The signal value $STATUS_TELEMATIC.PowerSts_Telematic$ = 1 (Standby) is received
```

**Remarks**

```
(R-P383)
```

5. No TLM, FPDM, AMP, ICS or DTV functionality is available

## `NR1L-PowerManagement-277`　`SWE-PM-007`　Bench turns the AMP, ICS and DTV on for the Engineering Line

**Test Item**

```
This status is related to TLM AMP, ICS, and DTV ON only for testing, diagnostics and development of TLM component, relatively to Engineering Line.

(read the TLM, AMP, ICS and DTV states -> The TLM, AMP, ICS and DTV are ON)
```

**Pre-Conditions**

```
1. A LIN and CAN simulation tool is connected
2. The ignition working condition is Ignition Off
3. The Engineering Line is activated
```

**Input Test Data**

```
NA
```

**Test procedure**

```
1. Bring the TLM to the Bench state
2. Apply FUNC_STATE_Bench and check all of its sub-items
3. Read the HU speakers and check whether entertainment audio output is present on them
4. Touch the screen and read the bus trace, and check whether $TELEMATIC_FD_5.CM_TCH_STAT$ is 1 (TCH_PSD) with coordinates
5. Apply FUNC_STATE_Bench and check its Display sub-item  (DTV 影像僅經顯示可見；規格無獨立 DTV 觀察面)
```

**Expected Result**

```
1. The TLM reaches the Bench state
2. The TLM, AMP, ICS and DTV are ON
3. The TLM, AMP, ICS and DTV are ON
4. The TLM, AMP, ICS and DTV are ON
5. The TLM, AMP, ICS and DTV are ON
```

**Remarks**

```
(R-P394 B5；規則 無) (R-P396 字典套用)
```

## `NR1L-PowerManagement-278`　`SWE-PM-009`　The ex-factory defaults are applied on the first power up

**Test Item**

```
First default values for TLM are: TLM_Status.Info, $Telematic_Power$ equal to "Sleep" value; VPLastStatus equal to "On" value; SwitchOff_Timeout_Setting.Req equal to "00 min" == Timeout1 equal to 00 minutes; Timeout1 equal to 00 minutes for LTM High Auto_SwitchOn_Setting.Req equal to "Recall_Last" value; Antitheft_Activation.Req equal to "False" value.

(read TLM_Status.Info, VPLastStatus, SwitchOff_Timeout_Setting.Req, Auto_SwitchOn_Setting.Req, Antitheft_Activation.Req and RemStartFail -> VPLastStatus reads "On", SwitchOff_Timeout_Setting.Req reads "00 min", Auto_SwitchOn_Setting.Req reads "Recall_Last", Antitheft_Activation.Req reads "False" and RemStartFail reads "False")
```

**Pre-Conditions**

```
1. A LIN and CAN simulation tool is connected
2. The unit carries the ex-factory configuration
```

**Input Test Data**

```
NA
```

**Test procedure**

```
1. Power up the TLM for the first time
2. Read the signal $STATUS_TELEMATIC.PowerSts_Telematic$ and check that it is the expected 0 (Sleep)
3. PENDING: DR-PW23 VPLastStatus
4. PENDING: DR-PW23 SwitchOff_Timeout_Setting.Req
5. PENDING: DR-PW25 Auto_SwitchOn_Setting.Req 之設定項名
6. PENDING: DR-PW23 Antitheft_Activation.Req
7. PENDING: DR-PW23 RemStartFail
```

**Expected Result**

```
1. TLM_Status.Info and $Telematic_Power$ read "Sleep"
2. PENDING: DR-PW23 SwitchOff_Timeout_Setting.Req
3. PENDING: DR-PW23 SwitchOff_Timeout_Setting.Req
4. PENDING: DR-PW23 SwitchOff_Timeout_Setting.Req
5. PENDING: DR-PW23 SwitchOff_Timeout_Setting.Req
6. PENDING: DR-PW23 SwitchOff_Timeout_Setting.Req
7. PENDING: DR-PW23 SwitchOff_Timeout_Setting.Req
```

**Remarks**

```
(R-P394 B5；規則 R4) (R-P396 字典套用)
```

## `NR1L-PowerManagement-279`　`SWE-PM-009`　A battery disconnection puts the TLM into the INIT state

**Test Item**

```
IF the voltage exceeds the higher or the lower voltage threshold for a certain time, or also at every battery disconnection event,THEN TLM has to set itself in a INIT state, until certain conditions that allow TLM to exit from this status occur.

(read the TLM state -> The TLM sets itself in the INIT state)
```

**Pre-Conditions**

```
1. A LIN and CAN simulation tool is connected
2. The TLM is running normally
```

**Input Test Data**

```
NA
```

**Test procedure**

```
1. Disconnect the battery
2. Read the signal $STATUS_TELEMATIC.PowerSts_Telematic$ and check that it is the expected <raw> (<STATE>)
```

**Expected Result**

```
1. The TLM registers the battery disconnection
2. The TLM sets itself in the INIT state
```

**Remarks**

```
(R-P394 B5；規則 無) (R-P396 字典套用) (站④-2：`<STATE>` 未能由本條推定，待分析層指定)
```

## `NR1L-PowerManagement-280`　`SWE-PM-009`　Leaving INIT restores the last settings and starts from Sleep

**Test Item**

```
After a battery reconnection and also when TLM has to exit INIT state (as soon as the voltage is limited within certain thresholds), TLM is able to work properly again and it has to restore the last user settings and the last variables values: VPLastStatus, SwitchOffSetting.Req, Auto_SwitchOn_Setting.Req shall be restored to their values before the battery disconnection / battery reset

(read TLM_Status.Info -> TLM_Status.Info is set to "Sleep" and the TLM starts from the Sleep state)
```

**Pre-Conditions**

```
1. A LIN and CAN simulation tool is connected
2. The TLM is in the INIT state after a battery disconnection
3. PENDING: DR-PW23 SwitchOffSetting.Req
```

**Input Test Data**

```
NA
```

**Test procedure**

```
1. Reconnect the battery and let the TLM exit the INIT state
2. PENDING: DR-PW23 SwitchOffSetting.Req
3. Read the signal $STATUS_TELEMATIC.PowerSts_Telematic$ and check that it is the expected 0 (Sleep)
```

**Expected Result**

```
1. The TLM exits the INIT state
2. PENDING: DR-PW23 SwitchOffSetting.Req
3. TLM_Status.Info is set to "Sleep" and the TLM starts from the Sleep state
```

**Remarks**

```
(R-P394 B5；規則 R4/R5) (R-P396 字典套用)
```

## `NR1L-PowerManagement-281`　`SWE-PM-007`　Bench powers the amplifier, booster, antennas and MCU on

**Test Item**

```
Bench
LTM plays the audio active source (Tuner, USB, AUX_IN or Phone Call, etc)
ON (Not muted)
ON DCSD follows behavior related to intensity and display status as defined in CFTS020 and VF668 DCSD sends touch coordinates
ON
ON Refer to {CFTS024} for further details about Antenna power supply
ON Refer to {VF654} for further details about Antenna power supply
ON (if present) Refer to {VF652} for further details about USB presence
ON (if present) Refer to {VF652} for further details about AUX_IN presence

(read the USB and AUX MCU states -> The USB and AUX MCU are ON when present)
```

**Pre-Conditions**

```
1. A LIN and CAN simulation tool is connected
The ignition working condition is Ignition Off
The Engineering Line is activated
TLM_Status.Info reads "Bench"
```

**Input Test Data**

```
NA
```

**Test procedure**

```
1. Apply ENTER_BENCH
2. Read the signal $STATUS_TELEMATIC.PowerSts_Telematic$ and check that it is 6 (Bench)
3. Read the HU speakers and check that the audio active source is playing on them
4. PENDING: DR-PW27 BoosterOUT / analog and digital antenna supply 之 ON 位準值 —— measure the voltage at each output and check it against that level
5. Insert a USB device and check that it is enumerated and can be played, and check that the AUX input plays on the HU speakers
```

**Expected Result**

```
1. The HU is in Bench
2. The signal value $STATUS_TELEMATIC.PowerSts_Telematic$ = 6 (Bench) is received
3. The audio active source is playing on the HU speakers
4. PENDING: DR-PW27 BoosterOUT / antenna supply ON 位準值
5. The USB device is enumerated and can be played, and the AUX input plays on the HU speakers
```

**Remarks**

```
(R-P386 第二批)
```

## `NR1L-PowerManagement-284`　`SWE-PM-075`　A dismissed FOTA pop up passes the TLM to Standby

**Test Item**

```
If the HU Transitions to Timed mode due to the condition described in CFTS009-1809, the HU shall transition to Standby mode after: -1 minute has passed without the user interacting with the pop-up -The FOTA pop up is dismissed -$ACCDlyAct$ transitions from active to inactive

(read the HU mode after the idle period -> The HU transitions to Standby mode after 1 minute has passed)
```

**Pre-Conditions**

```
1. A LIN and CAN simulation tool is connected
2. The HU is in Timed mode due to the condition described in CFTS009-1809
```

**Input Test Data**

```
NA
```

**Test procedure**

```
1. PENDING: DR-PW27 CFTS057 —— FOTA update available 之建立方法
2. Apply ENTER_TIMED
3. Read the HU screen and check that the "FOTA update available" pop-up is shown
4. Dismiss the "FOTA update available" pop-up through the HMI, then read the HU screen and check that it is no longer shown on it
5. Read the signal $STATUS_TELEMATIC.PowerSts_Telematic$ and check that it is 1 (Standby)
```

**Expected Result**

```
1. PENDING: DR-PW27 CFTS057
2. The HU is in Timed
3. The "FOTA update available" pop-up is shown
4. The pop-up is no longer shown on the HU screen
5. The signal value $STATUS_TELEMATIC.PowerSts_Telematic$ = 1 (Standby) is received
```

**Remarks**

```
(R-P393(c) §8.3 拆分增列；本條與 NR1L-PowerManagement-169 同錨點)
```

## `NR1L-PowerManagement-285`　`SWE-PM-075`　An accessory delay going inactive passes the TLM to Standby

**Test Item**

```
If the HU Transitions to Timed mode due to the condition described in CFTS009-1809, the HU shall transition to Standby mode after: -1 minute has passed without the user interacting with the pop-up -The FOTA pop up is dismissed -$ACCDlyAct$ transitions from active to inactive

(read the HU mode after the idle period -> The HU transitions to Standby mode after 1 minute has passed)
```

**Pre-Conditions**

```
1. A LIN and CAN simulation tool is connected
2. The HU is in Timed mode due to the condition described in CFTS009-1809
```

**Input Test Data**

```
NA
```

**Test procedure**

```
1. PENDING: DR-PW27 CFTS057 —— FOTA update available 之建立方法
2. Apply ENTER_TIMED with $BCM_FD_27.Comfort_Enable_Act$ = 1 (DR-PW26)
3. Read the HU screen and check that the "FOTA update available" pop-up is shown
4. Send the signal $BCM_FD_27.Comfort_Enable_Act$ = 0 (DR-PW26)
5. Read the signal $STATUS_TELEMATIC.PowerSts_Telematic$ and check that it is 1 (Standby)
```

**Expected Result**

```
1. PENDING: DR-PW27 CFTS057
2. The HU is in Timed
3. The "FOTA update available" pop-up is shown
4. The signal value $BCM_FD_27.Comfort_Enable_Act$ = 0 is received
5. The signal value $STATUS_TELEMATIC.PowerSts_Telematic$ = 1 (Standby) is received
```

**Remarks**

```
(R-P393(c) §8.3 拆分增列；本條與 NR1L-PowerManagement-169 同錨點)
```

**Test Item**

```
If $VC_VEH_LINE$ = [M240] The HU Shall use the M240 seat graphics.  If $VC_VEH_LINE$ <> [M240]The HU shall use $VC_VEH_BRAND$ to determine the correct settings seat graphic display.

(read the shown seat graphic against the brand signal -> The HU uses $VC_VEH_BRAND$ to determine the settings seat graphic)
```

2. The seat settings screen is reachable
3. Set VC_VEH_BRAND = the configured brand (DR-PW28)
```

**Input Test Data**

```
NA
```

**Test procedure**

```

2. Read the seat settings screen and check that the "M240 seat graphics" are shown on it
```

**Expected Result**

2. The "M240 seat graphics" are shown on the seat settings screen
```

```

## `NR1L-PowerManagement-287`　`SWE-PM-093`　A start-up animation plays again in the next wakeup cycle

**Test Item**

```
While HU is in SLEEP MODE, STANDBY MODE, or in PARTIAL OPERATION MODE and when driver door ajar status ($Door_Ajar_Status$) is changed to CLOSED, HU shall play a start-up animation as defined per HMI. The startup animation will be unique for each vehicle or/and brand as defined per PDO/HMI. If driver door is not present or removed for current vehicle ($DriverDoorOnOffSts$ = [DOOR_OFF]), or if HU changes mode (due to ignition event or to due to HU power mode status change to BODY ON or to TIMED MODE while driver door ajar status ($Door_Ajar_Status$) is OPEN, the HU shall skip start-up animation. While HU is playing a start-up animation and HU changes mode (due to ignition event or to HU power mode status changes to BODY ON or to TIMED MODE) or if an ignition crank event ($PowerMode$ = [IGN_START ]), then HU shall cancel current start-up animation and switch required power mode as defined. Once a start-up animation is played, HU shall not play the next start-up animation until the next CAN wakeup cycle OR at least 30 minutes passed from last time the start-up animation was played ;whichever is greater; given all other conditions are met for startup animation to play as defined here.

(read the screen against the elapsed time -> A start-up animation plays again only at the next CAN wakeup cycle or after 30 minutes, whichever is greater)
```

**Pre-Conditions**

```
1. A LIN and CAN simulation tool is connected
2. The HU has just played a start-up animation
3. All other conditions for the animation hold
```

**Input Test Data**

```
NA
```

**Test procedure**

```
1. Set Door_Ajar_Status = "Open" then "Closed" again within the same CAN wakeup cycle (DR-PW28)
2. Read the HU screen and check that the "Start-up Animation" is not played on it
3. PENDING: DR-PW26 Sleep 態之觀察方法 —— let the Body CAN go to sleep and wake it again to start a new wakeup cycle
4. Repeat the door event and read the HU screen, and check that the "Start-up Animation" is played on it
```

**Expected Result**

```
1. The door event is registered within the same CAN wakeup cycle
2. The "Start-up Animation" is not played
3. PENDING: DR-PW26 Sleep 態之觀察方法
4. The "Start-up Animation" is played on the HU screen in the new wakeup cycle
```

**Remarks**

```
(R-P393(c) §8.3 拆分增列；本條與 NR1L-PowerManagement-182 同錨點)
```
